## intake-938#record — MemHarness: Memory Is Reconstructed, Not Replayed

- **URL**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.28272
- **Verification**: dive-overturned
- **Verdict**: adopt_patterns (novelty medium, relevance medium; categories: memory_augmented, agent_architecture, training_distillation, context_management)

### Claims (as verified)

0. Verbatim replay of retrieved experience causes negative transfer; memory must be adapted to the current state before injection.
   - *narrowed*: Verbatim-replay harm tracks ENTITY-BEARING memory — WebShop's entity-free memories yield no benefit; the adapt-before-inject remedy is only +2.2pp, z ~ 1.94, NOT significant.
1. A single unified policy performs both reconstruction and action; the reconstructive ability is not supervised but emerges from end-to-end GRPO.
   - *unaffected*: Supported: there is NO critique step and NO <EMPTY> criterion, which is also why it fails zero-shot — all discrimination lives in the weights; <EMPTY> is a learned state-comparison.
2. Naively combining external memory with RL is actively HARMFUL, not merely neutral: Mem0+GRPO 52.0 and SimpleMem+GRPO 54.5 vs plain GRPO 76.4 on ALFWorld Avg SR.
   - *unaffected*: The control fix targets the reconstruction arm and the EvolveR row, not the Mem0/SimpleMem cells; negative transfer (+6.25pp, z=4.39) does survive sampling noise.
3. LATENT GUIDANCE (the most stack-relevant claim): training against a reconstruction objective improves the agent intrinsic memory-free policy, so gains persist when memory is removed at inference.
   - *unaffected*: Quantified and confirmed dominant: of the +8.8/+9.5 over GRPO, +6.6/+7.5 is a TRAINING effect (83.0 vs 76.4, both memory-free at test), z=5.20.
4. Zero-shot text rewriting is insufficient - the reconstruction competence must be trained into the policy.
   - *unaffected*: Strengthened: THERE IS NO GRADIENT-FREE WIN ANYWHERE IN THE PAPER (untrained reconstructor 77.7 vs 83.0 without memory). EvolveR's gradient-free win is a different mechanism.

### Dive corrections

- 2026-08-03 DIVE-F. OVERTURNED on several load-bearing points; the mechanism description survives. (1) THE CONTROL WAS WRONG. The correct comparator for `generic LLM reconstruction` is `w/o memory`, not `RL + Raw Memory` - both use the SAME MemHarness-trained actor and differ only in the reconstructor. Against it the untrained reconstructor scores 77.7 vs 83.0 (ALFWorld) and 71.8 vs 73.6 (WebShop): WORSE THAN NO MEMORY AT ALL on both. THERE IS NO GRADIENT-FREE WIN ANYWHERE IN THE PAPER. (2) The cold-start datum Stage 1 declared unavailable was in Table 2 all along, in two rows this entry omitted: `Base Model` 14.5/7.8 and `Cold Start Model` 7.6/17.6 - the latter BELOW the un-fine-tuned base on ALFWorld. Both SFT-only checkpoints are downloadable; they are format-aligned shells, not policies. (3) DECOMPOSITION: of the +8.8/+9.5 over GRPO, +6.6/+7.5 is a TRAINING effect (83.0 vs 76.4, both memory-free at test) and only +2.2/+2.0 is test-time memory. A gradient-free port targets a ~2pp effect. On ALFWorld Pick the full system is 13.0pp BELOW the memory-free control. (4) WebShop Score/SR were INVERTED in this entry. Table 1 header is `Avg. SR | Score | SR`; correct reading is SR 75.6 / Score 87.4. Confirmed three ways incl. Sec 4.2 "success rates of ... 75.6% on WebShop". (5) The Table 3 OOD `w/o memory 83.0` row is BYTE-IDENTICAL to Table 2 across all six categories and the average, while every other row differs; ALFWorld ID=140 vs OOD=134 games make identical percentages essentially impossible (Look 68.4 = 13/19 exactly). Plain reading: carried over, not re-run. RETRACT "raw memory falls 6.7pp BELOW memory-free under distribution shift" - it compares an OOD number against an in-distribution one, a gate-scope category error. (6) CREDIBILITY 3 -> 2. The +1 for "independent corroboration of the MECHANISM (intake-899#record, intake-935#record)" is UNEARNED: intake-899#record's mechanism is the consolidation/rewriting schedule and its own claim 4 states raw episodic injection is FINE; intake-935#record's is heuristic curation x weak executor, with MemP ABOVE control in 7 of 12 cells. Four distinct mechanisms, not one replicated finding. Compounding: the `RL + Raw Memory` baseline IS EvolveR (arXiv 2510.16079), first author Rong Wu, the SAME first author - self-reproduced. (7) THE SkillOS CONTRADICTION DOES NOT EXIST. It was a baseline-mismatch artifact: MemHarness's gradient-free arm loses to `w/o memory` on both; SkillOS-base BEATS no-memory on both (53.1 vs 47.9, 63.6 vs 61.2, 38.6 vs 33.3, 43.4 vs 41.5). The intake-935#record flip is SkillOS-base vs ReasoningBank, a different comparison. DELETE contradicting_evidence item 2 and recommended_action 4. (8) THE REAL POLARITY, genuine and favourable to us: verbatim-replay harm tracks ENTITY-BEARING memory. ALFWorld memories drag concrete entities into the wrong task (Fig 12: "credit card"/"coffee table" into a cup/sidetable task) so replay emits an INVALID ACTION -> 8.7% rejection, large win. WebShop memories are entity-free heuristics -> 72.1% rejection even on APPLICABLE memory, no benefit. Our autopilot/trace memory is heavily entity-bearing (file paths, config keys, flag values, PIDs, commit SHAs), placing us on the ALFWorld side. (9) COST PREMISE REVERSED. Reconstruction fires only on retrieval steps (`schedule: on_memory_only`), 1-5 times per EPISODE, not before every action; and the adaptor output REPLACES the memory block, deleting it entirely on <EMPTY>. Three retrieved raw memories become one short sentence or nothing - NET CHEAPER on context than raw injection. Agent-estimated decode overhead ~4% ALFWorld / ~5.5% WebShop. THE REAL COST IS KV-CACHE PREFIX THRASH: the reconstruction call is a separate prompt with a different prefix, so a naive single-slot port forces a cold 270-880-token prefill per event and evicts the agent's cached prefix. Slot topology must be decided up front. (10) techniques CORRECTED: retrieval top-k=3 but only TOP-1 is reconstructed (`mem_adaptor.retrieval_top_k: 1`). (11) The prompts ARE fully reproduced (Appendix C Figs 7-8) and match the repo config - this entry said they were not. But there is NO critique step and NO <EMPTY> criterion: the five-stage narrative collapses to one subordinate clause, which is also why it fails zero-shot - all discrimination lives in the weights. (12) The "<EMPTY> is shallow and may transfer untrained" inference is OVERTURNED: invariance holds only for source REMOVAL (8.7->7.8); CORRUPTING the source moves rejection sharply (8.7->13.3, 56.0->63.3). It is a learned state-comparison. The surviving reason to port it first is different and stronger: it strictly reduces injected tokens and is fail-closed. (13) Compute FOUND in repo: 8 GPUs x 1 node, 200 steps, 128 parallel envs; paper says TP=1 but both scripts set 2. Licence SPLIT: GitHub Apache-2.0, README badge and HF card MIT. Undisclosed reward term `use_invalid_action_penalty` coef 0.1. (14) No independent replication or criticism exists (4 days old; HF downloads 0, likes 0; GitHub 14 stars, untouched since 2026-07-31). All figures are OBSERVATION-grade. STAGE-2b ADDENDUM 2026-08-03 (dive 2b-G, memory cluster) - three corrections, one deletion. DELETE THE SAMPLE SIZES. The n=140/250 recorded in this entry is STATED NOWHERE IN THE PAPER. It was an inference, and it is wrong: MemHarness's ALFWorld cells CANNOT be single-pass success rates. No common per-category denominator <=200 reproduces the reported decimals across the six Table 2 rows; the minimum consistent denominators are 455/304/400/336/360/190, i.e. roughly 2,045 episode outcomes. No repeats, no seeds and no variance are disclosed. THE EvolveR BASELINE REPRODUCES NOTHING PUBLISHED. EvolveR (arXiv 2510.16079, now intake-988#record) NEVER evaluates on ALFWorld or WebShop - it is a 7-benchmark multi-hop QA paper. "70.1" appears ZERO times; ALFWorld and WebShop appear ZERO times; the only "embodied" mention is in Future Work. So the "EvolveR (reproduced) 70.1" row is not a reproduction of any published number - THERE IS NO ORIGINAL TO DIVERGE FROM, and the label "(reproduced)" asserts fidelity to a non-existent original. Eight shared authors including the same first author (Rong Wu) and the same seniors; both Shanghai AI Lab. THE ARM WE WOULD HAVE PORTED IS THE WEAKEST EFFECT IN THE PAPER. The +2.2pp test-time memory effect - exactly what a gradient-free port would target - is z ~ 1.94 at the most favourable denominators, i.e. NOT significant on one seed with no CI. The training effect (+6.68pp, z=5.20) and negative transfer (+6.25pp, z=4.39) DO survive sampling noise; neither is protected against SEED variance, which is unmeasured. The reconstruct-before-inject arm is KEPT BUT DEMOTED. DARK ROOM DOES NOT THREATEN THIS ENTRY (intake-990#record). The collapse mechanism requires a dense policy-dependent potential-difference shaping term with persistent within-group variance; MemHarness has sparse +10/0 plus a saturating 0.1x format bonus plus SFT format cold-start, which is Dark Room's own Proposition-2 SAFE class. Decisively, Dark Room's own NON-COLLAPSING BASELINE IS MemHarness's reward shape, on the same verl-agent framework. It does destabilise on MEASUREMENT: 32-episode binomial noise floor ~8.8 points, "all cross-arm comparisons in this version are DESCRIPTIVE", and an unexplained 8B (32.8) below 4B (49.5). CREDIBILITY 2 -> 2, COMPOSITION CHANGES: -1 (the EvolveR baseline reproduces nothing published) +1 (independent corroboration from "Honest Lying", intake-989#record, FOR CLAIM A ONLY - memory injection can drive below a no-memory control, by a controlled memory-WIPE ablation, independent group, no citation either direction. NOT corroboration for the state-mismatch MECHANISM, which is write-side vs read-side - a FOURTH distinct mechanism). HIGHER-VALUE LEAD THAN THIS ENTRY'S OWN: EvolveR Table 4 contains a GRADIENT-FREE WIN MemHarness never reports - on Qwen2.5-3B, principle distillation + retrieval with RL DISABLED scores 0.357, BEATING RL-only at 0.325 and reaching 93.5% of the full system's 0.382. See intake-988#record.

### Verdict

adopt_patterns. Not adopt_component - the artifact is a GRPO pipeline over verl-agent + vLLM 0.8.4 + Milvus and we are an inference-only llama.cpp stack with one MI210 and no RL training infrastructure. Not not_applicable - intake-930#record is already adopted in this exact area. Not superseded - nothing in the index dominates it. The portable element is a prompt-level architecture: critique-and-rewrite between retrieval and injection, with an explicit rejection path.

### Linked handoffs and actions

- updated `handoffs/active/engram-conditional-memory.md`
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Add reconstruct-before-inject as a SECOND arm alongside the adopted ReasoningBank baseline in unified-trace-memory-service.md - prompt-level, no training, no inference budget.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Port the <EMPTY> rejection path first: the source-state ablation shows rejection rate is INVARIANT to removing the source observation (only task success moves, 85.2 -> 80.0), hinting rejection is a shallower competence than adaptation and may transfer to an untrained policy.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Log as a THIRD independent data point for the finding that naive memory injection can score BELOW no-memory (with intake-899#record and intake-935#record). Across three papers this looks like a property of memory-augmented agents rather than a per-paper artifact, and it argues for a MANDATORY no-memory control arm in any trace-reuse eval we run.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Record the domain-conditionality contradiction against intake-935#record so no one imports a ranking from either paper - only a hypothesis to test on our own workload.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Route the latent-guidance result to the skillbank-distillation.md owner: memory as a TRAINING-TIME scaffold discarded at serve time costs zero on the inference path, which fits a frozen-kernel stack far better than a live retrieval sidecar.

## intake-939#record — Reinforcement Learning for Code Optimization

0. Naively adding runtime to an RLVR reward does essentially nothing (+0.6 points pass@1 at p30) - the failure is in measurement and reward plumbing, not in the idea.

### Dive corrections

- 2026-08-03 DIVE-E (full 125pp incl. Appendices A-G, all 46 tables, plus a rendered read of Figure 8). Every load-bearing measurement number verified exactly. verdict adopt_patterns and credibility_score 4 both STAND. (1) ARTIFACT GATE RESOLVED NEGATIVE AND CLOSED. Sec C.3 verbatim: "CES is a pre-existing remote execution backend ... building the service itself is not a contribution of this paper" and "outside the scope of this paper" - it is internal Meta infrastructure. Zero release language in 125 pages; no Data Availability or Reproducibility section; Table 46 lists licences of CONSUMED assets only; the only own-group repo cited is facebookresearch/BigOBench (CC-BY-NC, fetched and verified to contain no DMC-Optim and no CES); no DMC-Optim on HuggingFace. adopt_component is UNREACHABLE - mark resolved-negative so no future pass re-opens it. USABLE ANYWAY: the paper text is CC BY 4.0, so Appendices A and C may be quoted at length into MEASUREMENT.md with attribution; the source corpus (CodeContests) is Apache-2.0, so the construction recipe is reproducible from open inputs. (2) THE ABSTRACT IS CONTRADICTED BY THE PAPER'S OWN FIGURE. Figure 8 bottom row ("Any complexity improves", N=174/177/154) reads 13 (vs adversary 2), 22 (vs 6), 16 (vs 7). Figure 8, Sec 6 and the Conclusion all agree on 13 vs 22. The abstract's 14/28 APPEARS NOWHERE ELSE. Two further defects in that sentence: its "relative to the fastest correct human submissions" framing is misleading (both figures are against RLVR as the common adversary; the true head-to-head row is 16 vs 7), and "about half" (14/28=50%) contradicts the body's "more than half" (13/22=59%). CITE 13 vs 22 WITH Figure 8 AND N; NEVER QUOTE 14/28. Judge caveats: speed-identification accuracy 68% overall and 59% on human-vs-model pairs; 22% of speed wins unclassifiable and excluded. (3) THE TABLE 4 REGRESSION IS REAL, and newly corroborated on a second benchmark. Qwen 2.5 32B / pre-execution absolute filter tau=2s reads 48.2/31.2/19.2/6.3 against Standard RLVR 54.8/21.1/9.4/2.4 - p100 is -6.6 vs RLVR and -11.8 vs MC+opt.tests. Not a transcription artifact: the Qwen 7B tau=2s row matches Table 3's collapsed-binary cell digit for digit; only p100 is anomalous; and DECISIVELY, Table 5 shows the SAME arm dropping LCB pass@1 52.7 -> 45.6 (-7.1). Two benchmarks, same direction. This QUALIFIES the abstract's "while preserving pure-correctness scores": true for the reported-best post-execution configurations, FALSE for the pre-execution filtering family on Qwen 2.5 32B. (4) THE 41.2 pp WAS MISCHARACTERISED IN THIS ENTRY. Sec C.2: "Across 23 problems and 425 reruns, repeated local re-execution moved the mean-percentile score by 41.2 percentage points PEAK-TO-PEAK ON AVERAGE" (within-problem std 10.961pp, worst range 73.743pp). It is rerun-to-rerun instability of the LOCAL HARNESS measured with NO STATED ADDED LOAD - not a local-vs-CES delta and not a co-residency-under-load number. The separate contention experiment (724 jobs) reported COMPLETION failures, not timing swings. MISSING COMPARATOR that makes it quotable: CES mean-percentile std = 2.1 pp (Table 31). STRONGER UNFILED DIAGNOSTIC: local execution makes 81.1% of problems look duration-filterable vs 48.2% on CES; since robust CV is scale-invariant a uniform speedup cannot explain this - contended timing MANUFACTURES SPREAD. Also: at a 0.1s timeout local execution inflates pass@1 by 21.3x/32.8x/50.3x/20.0x, and under local timing the 7B BASELINE OUTRANKS the 7B optimization model - the ranking INVERTS. End-to-end, 7 of 8 local-execution optimization RL variants DIED (OOM then distributed-comm failures, 160-1620 of 5000 steps, 4-8x slower). (5) THE OUTER-GATE CLAIM IS PARTIAL. The winning arm is COLLAPSED - a hard conjunction R=(1+c~)(1+g)/2-1 in which a correct-but-slow program collapses to the FULL FAILURE REWARD (-1). The TWO-GATE form R=(1+c~)(1+g)/4-(1-c~)/2, which is the true analogue of our lexicographic axiom, is the RUNNER-UP and scores LOWER at strict percentiles (45.7/26.1/14.9/4.9 vs 46.9/29.2/17.4/5.7). Table 3 verified digit-for-digit. WHAT IT LICENSES: the ablated property is "NO SPEED CREDIT FOR AN INCORRECT CANDIDATE" (both gated arms have it, beating the three that do not at every percentile) - NOT that the outer gate specifically is best. Cite as: additive blending cost 7.8 pass@1 points against the best gated arm (46.9->39.1) and 6.6 against the outer-gate arm (45.7->39.1); a pure-speed objective collapsed the policy to 0.0 at all four percentiles. (6) APPENDIX A IS THE REUSABLE CORE. Noise model d_obs = d_true(1+eps_mult) + eps_add, with the decisive derived quantity the RELATIVE ADDITIVE BURDEN r_add = eps_add/d_true, anchored on the measured 53ms intercept: at 53ms the perturbation is at least HALF the observed duration for 75.9% of LCB and 52.4% of DMC-Optim executions. Three conclusions in the authors' order: additive noise is the right abstraction for fast suites; FIX THE DATA REGIME FIRST (threshold tuning cannot rescue a fast-test regime - LCB timeout pass@1 spans only 1.0 point, 29.6->30.6, across ALL training configs); and relative aggregation survives longer than a hard timeout, with timeout falling to RANDOM GUESSING across the whole sweep under additive noise while win-rate stays clearly above. The 17-metric screen (Sec D.3) selects PLAIN MEAN per-test percentile; TRIMMING AND FILTERING HURT. (7) THE C.8/C.9 COUPLING is the real argument for recalibration, and is stronger than "Spearman 0.54->0.96": across two NOMINALLY IDENTICAL campaigns on fixed infrastructure, alpha moved 1.0% (0.6306->0.6243) while BETA MOVED 28% (53ms->38ms); and at strict percentiles BETA IS THE DOMINANT LEVER - reducing it 0.0498->0.0344s collapses baseline p10 pass@1 from 38.8% to 1.2%. The parameter that drifts most under unchanged conditions is the one the strict metric is most sensitive to. HONESTY NOTE: stricter calibration WIDENS the optimization-RL advantage (45%->141%->233%), so miscalibration in the strict direction INFLATES apparent wins. (8) TRANSFER TO GPU KERNEL TIMING, honestly scoped. TRANSFERS: Appendix A's regime argument (eps_add maps to kernel-launch/host-dispatch, arguably a better fit than CPU) and timeout->percentile. TRANSFERS WITH REAL RISK: the affine drift correction - their alpha/beta was fit across one runtime on one CPU fleet where a fleet change plausibly IS a global scale-plus-offset, whereas a ROCm/LLVM codegen change on gfx90a can alter register allocation and occupancy for one kernel and not another. The paper hands us the exact test: leave-one-KERNEL-out CV across a ROCm epoch boundary; high CV R2 means adopt, negative CV R2 means cross-epoch results are unrecoverable and must be re-executed. CARRY THE PROTOCOL AND THE FALSIFICATION CRITERION, NOT alpha=0.6306. PARTIAL: robust CV >= 0.3 - their definition is spread across TESTS within a problem, deliberately not across candidate solutions, for a reason (fear of bias from an incidental human solution pool) that DOES NOT BIND US since we generate our own variants; and given bandwidth-bound decode the across-shape form passes trivially, so compute BOTH and treat the across-variant spread as the discriminative test. DOES NOT TRANSFER: binary > bucketed > continuous - the stated mechanism is gradient-specific ("before it reaches the gradient steps") and a binary speed signal destroys the magnitude a hill-climbing search needs. DECLINE the "test coarse buckets" action as filed. (9) 2605.28751 shares FOUR of five authors, not three (Chambon, Zheng, Decugis, Synnaeve; only Sagot absent). Non-independence is STRONGER than filed. credibility 4 is unaffected because the corroboration came from intake-949#record and intake-950#record, both disjoint. (10) Claim 1's qualifier is load-bearing: "+0.6pp does nothing" is true only on the BASE test regime; on OPTIMIZATION tests the same naive reward gives +3.2/+3.4 at p30. (11) SIMULATOR CEILING: diagnostics are NON-PREDICTIVE at p100 (all p>0.11) and only predictive at p30; variance-around-trend is useless everywhere (r_s=-0.002, p=0.994). Authors: "still unclear whether offline diagnostics can reliably rank the remaining viable configurations." (12) THIS PAPER REPEATS THE SWE-fficiency Sonnet FIGURE in Sec 1 and Sec 7. NOTE THE CHAIN: a Stage-1 pass flagged it as a row-crossing error, and DIVE-D then OVERTURNED THAT CORRECTION - SWE-fficiency v1 Table 2 lists Claude 4.5 Sonnet at 0.041x and Gemini 2.5 Flash at 0.008x, so this paper's citation is a FAITHFUL v1 QUOTE ON BOTH LEGS. The figure moved to 0.116x only in v3 via an aggregation change. Quote it version-pinned to v1 or not at all. (13) NO independent evaluation, replication or criticism exists (paper 6 days old). The only secondary coverage contains a garbled figure and is explicitly NOT a source. (14) Fallback policy worth porting (Sec C.4-C.6): a locally-recovered success on a TIMING test is reclassified as a TIMEOUT at the limit; on a CORRECTNESS test the verdict is kept but the DURATION IS NEVER IMPORTED, because "a local rerun under different pressure can return a FASTER time for a generation that was WORSE on the real backend - importing that local runtime would reverse the signal." Eq.9 exposure: 13.9% of rollouts touch fallback at f=0.1%, n=150. STAGE-2b ADDENDUM 2026-08-03 (dive 2b-E, RLPF independence test; dive 2b-H, measurement sources). KEY_CLAIM 6 MUST BE NARROWED. It currently reads "Correctness must gate the speed signal as an OUTER gate; additive or blended reward is actively harmful, and a pure-speed objective collapses the policy outright." Three separate corrections: (a) "as an OUTER gate" was ALREADY wrong - this paper's own winner is the COLLAPSED CONJUNCTION. RLPF (intake-974#record) now makes the stakes concrete by independently choosing the outer gate and REJECTING the conjunction BY DESIGN (epsilon = 0.05 exists solely to guarantee every correct program outranks every failed one). REWRITE to the property both share: "No speed credit for an incorrect candidate. WHICH gate form is best is UNSETTLED - 2607.25970 Table 3 favours the hard conjunction; 2607.27271 Eq. 6 independently chose a strictly-separated outer gate and never tested the conjunction." (b) "additive or blended reward is actively harmful" - UNCHANGED, still single-group. RLPF cannot test it: EVERY reward arm RLPF ran is correctness-gated, with no blended and no ungated arm anywhere in the paper or the code. (c) "a pure-speed objective collapses the policy" - still single-group, AND NOW NEEDS A GUARD so no future pass mistakes RLPF's "runtime-only" arm for it. That arm is GATED (its code comment: "CRR (correctness gating) is preserved by the `not correct` branch above") and it did NOT collapse (37.9% CRR vs 11.1% base). CREDIBILITY 4 UNCHANGED. Its +2 came from intake-949#record and intake-950#record, both disjoint. Adding RLPF would DOUBLE-COUNT a corroboration that is ARCHITECTURAL ONLY, and the rubric ceiling is reached regardless. STRENGTHENED FROM A DIFFERENT DIRECTION, AND THIS IS THE STRONGEST RESULT OF THE 2b-E DIVE. This entry's test-suite claim - robust CV >= 0.3 satisfied for <=3.8% of problems with original tests vs 48.2% with purpose-built ones - now has GENUINE INDEPENDENT EMPIRICAL CORROBORATION from arXiv 2607.07619 (intake-976#record): different group, different method (30 reps/task plus statistical testing across EffiBench, ENAMEL, EvalPerf and Mercury; 1,538 tasks), same conclusion - only 6.11% of purportedly performant reference implementations show a statistically significant improvement on the ORIGINAL tests, and purpose-built performance tests raise detection to 24-25%. That is corroboration for a DIFFERENT claim than the one the dive set out to test. THE PAIRED-CI CITATION IS FAITHFUL BUT ITS SOURCE IS SAME-LAB. arXiv 2512.21326 (intake-982#record) holds verbatim, and this entry's citation of it is properly hedged - and this entry does NOT itself apply the method, using the conservative unpaired inflation instead. BUT the author is FAIR at Meta AND a direct SWE-RL co-author of this entry's senior author. RESTATEMENT-CLASS SAME-LAB METHODOLOGY: adoptable as an instrument, NEVER countable as a corroborating leg for this entry. 2404.18864 (intake-984#record) COEXISTS, does NOT contradict: no binary-vs-continuous ablation exists in it, and it uses a different estimator. Notably its reward denominator is a STORED constant from a separate isolated campaign while the numerator is measured ON THE TRAINING NODE - exactly this entry's condemned channel, shipped independently. Venue upgrade: it is now IEEE TPDS 2026, DOI 10.1109/TPDS.2026.3675550.

### Verdict

Carry the four transferable patterns, chiefly: never time candidates on the machines running the search. Do NOT treat the GRPO post-training recipe as adoptable — it needs training GPUs we do not have. The remote-execution backend is explicitly out of the paper's own scope, so do not cite it as a released artifact.
adopt_patterns, not adopt_component. The primary contribution is a GRPO post-training recipe requiring training GPUs we do not have. But not_applicable would be wrong: four patterns transfer and are cheap - (1) never time candidates on the machines running the search, and note that no regression rescues a contended measurement; (2) correct sandbox drift affinely against a stored reference pool; (3) gate correctness first and keep the speed term binary or bucketed; (4) build an offline replay simulator to screen reward designs before spending the scarce serial resource. Not adopt_component because no releasable artifact was identified in this pass.

### Linked handoffs and actions

- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Evaluate the affine drift correction (Spearman 0.54 -> 0.96 against a fixed reference pool) as the mechanism for comparing MI210 kernel results banked weeks apart across ROCm, driver and host changes. autokernel-research-loop.md banks robust medians per fingerprint and has no cross-epoch recalibration; MEASUREMENT.md era handling is the natural home.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Adopt the duration-filterability gate (robust CV = IQR/median >= 0.3) as an admission test for any workload used to rank variants by speed - a direct analogue of the intake-664#record finding that too-small KernelBench tensors measure only launch overhead.

This is the first quantified, ablated evidence for our lexicographic-fitness axiom.

## intake-940#record — Frontis-MA1: Training an AI4AI Model towards Recursive Self-Improvement in Machine Learning Engineering

- **URL**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.28568
- **Verification**: dive-verified
- **Verdict**: adopt_patterns (novelty high, relevance high; categories: autonomous_research, agent_architecture, context_management, training_distillation, benchmark_methodology)

### Claims (as verified)

0. The improver itself is trained, not just the artifact: four atomic program-evolution operators (Draft, Improve, Debug, Crossover) are the shared interface between post-training and inference-time search.
   - *unaffected*: Structural claim. The dive opens by stating 'Structure, novelty framing and verdict adopt_patterns all HOLD', and the four-operator interface is not among the eight corrections.
   - anchor `matched span in https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.28568`: "On this stack we post-train Frontis-MA1 (35B) as a meta-evolution agent for MLE, aligning post-training and inference around four atomic program-evolution operators (Draft, Improve, Debug, Crossover): the same operators are trained via execution-grounded SFT and RL on data deduplicated against al..."
1. Model-side post-training gains and harness-side search gains are complementary and compose.
   - *overturned*: This is the compositionality claim and the dive nulls the comparisons that carry it. The harness swap 53.03 -> 60.61 rests on an AIRA-Evo row ABSENT FROM TABLE 9 ENTIRELY — the only comparison with no dispersion — and is Welch p~0.30 even granting the baseline zero variance; Evo -> Evo-Max on the same checkpoint is p=0.264. The statistical reconstruction is worse than null: one of the three headline runs scored 59.09%, BELOW the 68.18% it claims to beat and below Claude Opus 4.8 and Gemini 3.5 Flash from the same panel. Only the post-training-over-frozen-base result survives, and that is not this.
2. Non-greedy three-factor parent selection (quality / progress / novelty) preserves complementary hypotheses that score-only lineage search discards.
   - *narrowed*: The MECHANISM survives; the published WEIGHTS do not. Correction (6): released code sets score 1.0 / delta 0.4 / novelty 0.25 identically in two files, while 1.0/0.6/0.3 appears only in Sec 6.5 case-study prose — and reconstructing that case study, 10.47% -> 17.09% is consistent with lambda_delta ~0.6 (predicts ~17.6%) and NOT with the shipped 0.4 (~14.9%). Cite the three-factor structure, never the weights.
3. Operator-conditioned on-demand memory synthesis beats eager full-history serialization on both cost and yield; the saving comes primarily from making each expansion cheaper, not from terminating search earlier.
   - *narrowed*: 'On both cost AND yield' becomes cost-only. The COST half is credible engineering and the dive says so: tokens 129.3M -> 75.3M (-41.7%), prompt tokens -50.3%, Improve prompt p99 -86.1%, Crossover p99 -81.3%, matched checkpoint/seed/12h over 66 task-runs. The YIELD half is dead: correction (3) shows '+84.3% new-best per 1M tokens' decomposes as 1.0742 x 1.7171, so 88.3% of the log-ratio gain IS THE DENOMINATOR — it restates the cost claim and is not an independent yield result. Marked DO NOT CARRY.
4. Both components transfer out of the training benchmark, tested on a held-out scientific-reproduction benchmark.
   - *overturned*: Testing did happen; transfer was not demonstrated. NatureBench is n=10 so each task is 10pp, the model-swap delta is TWO TASKS, and best-case one-sided p=0.25. Nothing of 'both components transfer' survives at that resolution. Related and worth carrying: OpenMLE-Tasks ships ZERO BYTES of task data — checksums.sha256 has 0 CSV/parquet/npy and 0 paths under data/public or data/private — while the dataset README and docs/release.md both state 1,415 built task packages. All three statements are false against the shipped manifest.

### Dive corrections

- 2026-08-03 DIVE-G. Structure, novelty framing and verdict adopt_patterns all HOLD. credibility_score 1 KEPT and re-derived. Eight material corrections. (1) STATISTICAL RECONSTRUCTION - the dive's core contribution. Every mean+-SD row in Table 9 admits EXACTLY ONE integer medal-count triple out of 22; all triples in [0,22]^3 were brute-forced against both SD conventions and every row resolved UNIQUELY, always under the POPULATION-SD convention (sample-SD is excluded outright). Frontis-MA1-35B + Evo-Max = 13,17,17 -> per-run 59.09 / 77.27 / 77.27. Frontis-MA1-35B + Evo = 50.00 / 63.64 / 68.18. Base + Evo = 31.82 / 40.91 / 45.45. CONSEQUENCE: ONE OF THE THREE HEADLINE RUNS SCORED 59.09%, BELOW the 68.18% it claims to beat, and below Claude Opus 4.8 (63.64) and Gemini 3.5 Flash (63.64) from the same panel. Also, the paper's +- UNDERSTATES dispersion by 22.5% versus the unbiased sample SD (7.73 -> 9.46). And the official openai/mle-bench leaderboard reports SEM, not SD (its recurring +-1.52 is the SEM of a 3-seed 22-task run), so restated in the board's own convention this is 71.21 +- 6.06 SEM against typical entries at +-1.52 - roughly 4x less reproducible. (2) EXACTLY ONE CLAIM IS SUPPORTED: execution-grounded post-training improves an MLE-search harness over its own frozen base. 35B 39.39 -> 60.61 shows COMPLETE RANK SEPARATION (all three treated runs exceed all three control runs), exact one-sided permutation p=0.05 which is the FLOOR achievable at 3-vs-3; Welch p=0.040. 30B 34.85 -> 53.03 also separates completely, Welch p=0.013. Fisher-combined p ~ 0.018. Also credible as engineering measurements (matched checkpoint, seed and 12h budget over 66 task-runs per harness): tokens 129.3M->75.3M (-41.7%), prompt tokens -50.3%, Improve prompt mean 102.8K->35.7K chars, p99 389.0K->54.3K (-86.1%), Crossover p99 -81.3%. (3) UNSUPPORTED, and must never be cited: "exceeds GPT-5.5 + Codex by 3.03pp" - the baseline is n=1, has no error estimate, and is SELF-RUN (GPT-5.5+Codex is not on the official leaderboard); the margin is exactly TWO MEDAL OUTCOMES out of 66 task-runs (1/66 = 1.515pp) = 0.50 SEM; Clopper-Pearson on the n=1 baseline is [45.13, 86.14]. Harness swap 53.03->60.61 (the AIRA-Evo row is ABSENT FROM TABLE 9 ENTIRELY - the only comparison with no dispersion; Welch p~0.30 even granting the baseline zero variance). Evo->Evo-Max on the same checkpoint (p=0.264). ALL cross-vendor Evo comparisons (p=0.43 to 0.81). "+84.3% new-best per 1M tokens" decomposes as 1.0742 x 1.7171, i.e. 88.3% of the log-ratio gain IS THE DENOMINATOR - it restates the cost claim and is not an independent yield result; DO NOT CARRY IT. NatureBench transfer in both directions (n=10, each task = 10pp; the model-swap delta is 2 tasks, best-case one-sided p=0.25). (4) FOUR OVERTURNS, three of them of the dive brief's own premises. Appendix B.4 DOES quantify the async speedup: 1.91x (97.0 vs 50.8 minutes mean step time across 40 matched steps). Appendix C.4 does NOT contain the weight schedule or temperature - only functional forms; 1.0/0.6/0.3 appears solely in Sec 6.5 case-study prose. The OpenMLE-Gym contract is NOT general: it is evaluate(y_true: DataFrame, y_pred: DataFrame) -> float plus validate_submission, scoring a prediction file against data/private/test_answer.csv, and CANNOT EXPRESS A THROUGHPUT-MEASUREMENT TASK. And OpenMLE-Tasks ships ZERO BYTES OF TASK DATA. (5) THE ZERO-DATA FINDING, verified from the dataset's OWN integrity manifest. checksums.sha256 (42,078 entries) contains 0 CSV/parquet/zip/npy and 0 paths under data/public or data/private; extensions are .py 24,833, .json 11,507, .txt 5,730. task_index.jsonl holds exactly 5,758 records with 5,735 carrying an EMPTY download_url and package_bytes summing to 608.6 GB of REBUILD cost behind Kaggle auth. Meanwhile the dataset README states "Ready-to-use Task packages | Built data/public, data/private, and runtime scripts | 1,415" and "Each directory under tasks/ is already built", and docs/release.md states "full task-package data is released for 1,415 tasks". All three are FALSE against the shipped manifest. Their own Appendix E gives a peer a daggered tick and a footnote for exactly this class of partial release. (6) SELECTOR WEIGHTS: THE PAPER AND THE CODE DISAGREE. Released code sets DEFAULT_PARENT_UTILITY_WEIGHTS = score 1.0 / delta 0.4 / novelty 0.25 (identically in two files); 0.6 and 0.3 appear nowhere as selector weights. Reconstructing the case study, 10.47%->17.09% is consistent with lambda_delta ~ 0.6 (predicts ~17.6%) and NOT with the shipped 0.4 (predicts ~14.9%) - the case study used an UNRELEASED configuration. Full spec: direction-aware min-max score with ties at 0.5; progress = max(0, s_i - s_i^parent) against the STRONGEST parent, POSITIVE-ONLY; novelty = 1/sqrt(1+N_f) over an auto-detected method family; softmax(U/tau) with tau = 1.0 FIXED; the fourth term is DEAD (hardcoded to 0.0). Island structure is INACTIVE in every released profile (num_islands 1, migration_prob 0.0, initial_temp = final_temp = 1.0), so C.4's "for a sampled island" describes machinery switched OFF. Authors' own caveat (Sec 6.5): the end-to-end difference "should not be attributed to the three weights alone", and THERE IS NO SELECTOR-ONLY ABLATION ANYWHERE IN THE PAPER. (7) "NEGATIVE-EVIDENCE MARKING" IS NARROWER than this entry states: not an exclusion filter and not a typed field, but a RENDERING DISCIPLINE - a deterministic error_signature per card plus a board-level repeated_errors counter, rendered as a named prompt line. Failures still enter the context, as one compact typed line instead of raw prior-attempt text. (8) ARTIFACTS ARE REAL AND BETTER THAN STAGE 1 ASSUMED, with a licence trap. Frontis-MA1-35B is 71.93 GB BF16, ungated, 86 downloads; a Q4_K_M GGUF ships at 21.17 GB plus an F16 mmproj, tested on llama.cpp b9637 while our binary reports 10107. LICENCE IS CC BY-NC 4.0 (NON-COMMERCIAL) on all four model repos, the code and the SFT corpus - BUT BOTH GGUFs CARRY general.license = apache-2.0 IN THE HEADER KV, so anything auto-extracting licence from GGUF metadata reads the WRONG ANSWER. The SFT corpus is real (26,259 rows); RL traces are NOT released; the EVALUATION SANDBOX IS NOT RELEASED (client-only), which is a hard blocker for reproducing the tables; and their own docs/validation.md states "Neither validation independently reproduces paper tables." A full pull of all four model repos is 173.6 GB against 158 GB free. (9) THE BASE MODEL IS OUR PRODUCTION FRONTDOOR AND OUR FROZEN KERNEL ALREADY SUPPORTS IT. Base is Qwen/Qwen3.6-35B-A3B (Apache-2.0); Frontis-MA1-35B/config.json is semantically identical to it. production-consolidated-v8 already carries src/models/qwen35moe.cpp, the converter registration, LLAMA_VOCAB_PRE_TYPE_QWEN35 and PROJECTOR_TYPE_QWEN3VL - NO PR AND NO EXPERIMENTAL BRANCH NEEDED. The architecture is NOT a plain Qwen3 MoE: it is a hybrid SSM/attention MoE, 40 layers (30 linear_attention + 10 full_attention, every 4th), 256 experts top-8, vocab 248,320, max_pos 262,144, plus a 27-layer vision tower. (10) TABLE 11 IS NOT THE COMPLETE LEADERBOARD AUDIT ITS CAPTION CLAIMS: it omits MARS+ (78.79), PiEvolve (80.30), LoongFlow (77.27) and Disarray (90.91), all above 71.21%. The third-party numbers it DOES print all match openai/mle-bench exactly. Separately, MiniMax M2.7 + Claude Code is printed as 45.50%, which is not a multiple of 1/22. (11) NO independent replication or critique exists: 0 issues open AND closed, 0 PRs, discussions disabled, 6 forks with ZERO divergence, 0 HF discussions on all four model repos, and all coverage is abstract restatement. THE OFFICIAL LEADERBOARD HAS BEEN FROZEN TO NEW SUBMISSIONS SINCE 2026-04-24, so no verification path currently exists. (12) CITATION RULE, recommended: only two claims may ever be cited from this entry - the post-training gain (2/2 backbones, complete rank separation, joint p~0.018) and the prompt-tail reduction in a matched 66-task-run comparison. Everything else is UNSUPPORTED. DIVE CORRECTION 9 - 2026-08-03 (Stage-2b dive 2b-F, MLE-Bench contamination cluster). THE OMITTED COMPARATOR IS NOT HYPOTHETICAL, AND IT IS AHEAD. MLEvolve (intake-978#record) scored Lite 80.30% = 53/66 on the OFFICIAL leaderboard, pre-freeze, third-party accepted, with published grading reports and source available - 6 MEDAL OUTCOMES (9.09 pp) above this entry's 71.21% = 47/66 AT THE IDENTICAL 12h BUDGET, roughly 4x this entry's entire headline margin. It is also 3 medal outcomes BELOW ML-Master 2.0 (75.76% = 50/66, intake-413#record), which this paper CITES in its own reference list and which is likewise on the board. Table 11 omits EVERY top-10 board entry, comparing instead against a self-run n=1 GPT-5.5+Codex that has never appeared on the board. SO THE CLAIM IS NOT MERELY STATISTICALLY UNSUPPORTED - IT IS FACTUALLY BEHIND THE VERIFIED SOTA at matched wall-clock. REQUIRED SCOPE QUALIFIER, and it is a real one: this is NOT matched on compute or openness. This entry ran on ONE RTX 4090 capped at 12 GB VRAM with a 35B OPEN-WEIGHT backbone; MLEvolve on an H200 (141 GB) with a PROPRIETARY FRONTIER backbone. DEFENSIBLE STATEMENT: within its own compute class the post-training gain over its frozen base is real and separable; its frontier-competitiveness framing is not, and must never be cited. NEW ARITHMETIC CONSTRAINT, derived independently of the earlier brute-force reconstruction: ALL of this entry's Lite figures resolve as k/66 (39.39 = 26, 60.61 = 40, 71.21 = 47), confirming the 66-run denominator. NO LITE COMPARISON FROM THIS ENTRY MAY BE STATED FINER THAN 1.5152 pp - one medal outcome. GOVERNING CITATION RULE now in force for this entry and intake-413#record (see intake-977#record): any MLE-Bench number must be cited with its integer medal count out of 66, with the +-1.5152 pp quantum stated, and flagged leaderboard-verified or self-reported. No margin under ~3 medal outcomes (4.5 pp) may be described as a difference. Neither entry's numbers may gate keep/revert/promote without local re-measurement. CREDIBILITY 1 UNCHANGED. VERDICT adopt_patterns UNCHANGED.

### Verdict

Only TWO claims may ever be cited: the post-training gain over its own frozen base (2/2 backbones, complete rank separation, joint p~0.018) and the prompt-tail reduction in a matched 66-run comparison. NEVER cite the GPT-5.5+Codex margin, the harness-swap delta, Evo->Evo-Max, any cross-vendor comparison, the +84.3% yield figure, or NatureBench transfer. State every MLE-Bench number as an integer medal count out of 66 with the +-1.5152 pp quantum; no margin under ~3 medal outcomes is a difference. Its frontier-competitiveness framing is factually behind verified SOTA (intake-978#record) and must be scoped to its compute class.
adopt_patterns, not adopt_component and not new_opportunity. Against adopt_component: the checkpoint is specialized for Kaggle-style MLE program synthesis, nothing in our serving path; GGUF support for the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B backbone under FROZEN production-consolidated-v8 is unverified; and the harness is bound to OpenMLE-Gym sandbox and evaluator contract. Against not_applicable: squarely in-domain for the agent-harness and autopilot work area, at a compute envelope we can beat. Against superseded: the closest indexed items (intake-772#record, intake-413#record, intake-753#record) are all train-free, so there is no successor to name. The harvestable patterns are mechanism-level and stack-agnostic.

### Linked handoffs and actions

- updated `handoffs/active/architect-model-selection-bench.md`
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Port the three-factor parent selector (score / parent-relative gain / method-family novelty, weights 1.0/0.6/0.3) into the autopilot Pareto archive as an alternative to greedy pruning. The within-pool probability recomputation (10.47% -> 17.09%) is a cheap offline check replicable against archived autopilot generations with no inference.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Adopt negative evidence as a first-class field in the autopilot/strategy experience record. Our stores record wins; recording typed failures so they are NOT re-serialized into child context is the missing half.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Replace eager full-history context assembly with operator-conditioned on-demand synthesis in the delegation/tool-output path. The p99 prompt-length collapse (389K -> 54.3K characters) suggests the win is concentrated in the tail, which is where our context blowups actually happen.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Harvest the pre-execution LLM-judge reward-hack gate (reward -0.5, skip sandbox) for the autokernel loop, where our corpus already flags that 73.8% of KernelBench iterative optimizations show proxy gains without real gains.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Cost MLE-Bench Lite as a FEASIBLE local benchmark rather than an aspirational one - their entire evaluation ran at 12 GPU-hours per task on one 12 GB card.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Treat every number here as an OBSERVATION that does not gate keep/revert/promote without local re-measurement, per the treatment autopilot-continuous-optimization.md already applies to intake-772#record and intake-753#record.

## intake-941#record — Inkling - How to Run Locally (Unsloth Documentation)

- **URL**: https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/inkling
- **Verification**: dive-verified
- **Verdict**: worth_investigating (novelty high, relevance high; categories: local_inference, quantization, moe_optimization, multimodal, inference_serving, context_extension)

### Claims (as verified)

0. Inkling is a family of open-weight multimodal models from Thinking Machines Labs: a 975B-total/41B-active flagship and a 276B-total/12B-active small variant.
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-A, operator-scoped to a worker_general / architect_critic candidate assessment against the incumbent gemma4-26B-A4B.
   - anchor `matched span in https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/inkling`: "Inkling by Thinking Machines Labs are open-weight multimodal models consisting of: Inkling-Small the new 276B (12B active) model and the earlier 975B (41B) parameter model."
1. Text, image and audio input with text output; Apache-2.0; 1,048,576-token context window.
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-A, operator-scoped to a worker_general / architect_critic candidate assessment against the incumbent gemma4-26B-A4B.
   - anchor `matched span in https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/inkling`: "Licensed under Apache 2.0, the models support a 1M token context window, native multimodal input for text , image , and audio , and generates text as the output."
2. Targets coding, agentic and tool-calling workloads, with interleaved thinking and tool calling.
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-A, operator-scoped to a worker_general / architect_critic candidate assessment against the incumbent gemma4-26B-A4B.
3. reasoning_effort is exposed through the chat template as a continuous 0.00-0.99 knob (none=0, low=0.2, medium=0.7, high=0.9, xhigh/max=0.99).
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-A, operator-scoped to a worker_general / architect_critic candidate assessment against the incumbent gemma4-26B-A4B.
   - anchor `matched span in https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/inkling`: "It also supports reasoning efforts where reasoning_effort can be none = 0, low = 0.2, medium = 0.7, high = 0.9, xhigh = 0.99 and max = 0.99."
4. llama.cpp support requires a SPECIFIC un-merged pull request (#25731), not upstream master.
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-A, operator-scoped to a worker_general / architect_critic candidate assessment against the incumbent gemma4-26B-A4B. CONFIRMED and sharpened: #25731 is OPEN, isDraft TRUE, mergedAt null, REVIEW_REQUIRED, 4078 additions across 63 files — i.e. rejected upstream in its current form, which strengthens rather than weakens 'requires a specific un-merged PR, not upstream master'.
5. Unsloth Dynamic UD-* GGUFs are the recommended quant family; dynamic 1-bit is claimed to retain ~74.2% accuracy at 86% size reduction.
   - *reattributed*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-A, operator-scoped to a worker_general / architect_critic candidate assessment against the incumbent gemma4-26B-A4B. The accuracy-retention ladder is MIS-DESCRIBED TWICE. The metric is 'Top-1% Retained (Accuracy Recovery)' — a top-1 TOKEN-AGREEMENT recovery figure against BF16, NOT a benchmark score — and it is measured on the 975B INKLING FLAGSHIP, NOT on Inkling-Small. Any use of it to reason about Inkling-Small quantization is unsupported. The 1-bit figure is also a RANGE, 74.2%-77.4%, not the point value this claim states.

### Dive corrections

- 2026-08-03 DIVE-A, scoped by the operator to a worker_general / architect_critic candidate assessment against the incumbent gemma4-26B-A4B. (1) THE ACCURACY-RETENTION LADDER IS MIS-DESCRIBED IN THIS ENTRY, TWICE. The metric is "Top-1% Retained (Accuracy Recovery)" - a top-1 token-agreement recovery figure against BF16, NOT a benchmark score - and it is measured on the 975B INKLING FLAGSHIP, NOT on Inkling-Small. Any use of it to reason about Inkling-Small quantization is unsupported. The 1-bit figure is a RANGE, 74.2%-77.4%, not a point. (2) THE PR IS REJECTED UPSTREAM IN ITS CURRENT FORM. #25731 is OPEN, isDraft TRUE, mergedAt null, REVIEW_REQUIRED, 4078 additions across 63 files, last updated 2026-08-03. A llama.cpp MEMBER wrote "there's far too much sketchy CUDA stuff in here to get merged like that ... Need a clear CPU version first, then we can add CUDA kernels - you can keep a fork until that happens", quoting unexplained magic constants; a COLLABORATOR demanded a split into FOUR PRs; the author conceded and reverted it to draft. (3) MTP IS EXPLICITLY STRIPPED BY THE CONVERTER: conversion/inkling.py declares _SKIP_PREFIXES including "model.mtp." and filter_tensors() returns None for matches, so MTP weights NEVER REACH THE GGUF. Self-speculation is not available through this path. (4) A NEW GLOBAL OP with a serving-shaped performance cliff: GGML_OP_FLASH_ATTN_EXT_BANDED raises GGML_OP_COUNT to 102 and bumps the RPC protocol version. A CPU reference DOES exist, but the fast path is gated on flash_attn AND head_dim in {64,128} AND KV cache type in {F32,F16,BF16}, with the author noting "get_n_kv_pos_contiguous() is 0 for multi-sequence ubatches". On our stack that means the fast path is OFF for multi-slot serving, for quantized KV, and for -fa 0 (llama-bench's default); the fallback materialises a dense per-layer bias across 42 layers. (5) INDEPENDENT NEGATIVE PERFORMANCE EVIDENCE, the strongest datum in the dive: a third party profiling the flagship on 8xA100 reported decode at ~25% of comparable-MoE throughput - inkling UD-Q4_K_XL 6.45 t/s against GLM-5.2-744B 24.0 t/s on identical boxes - with "All 8 GPUs sit at 0 to 6 percent utilization", graphs reused = 0 on every request, an 8985-node graph, and no change from GGML_CUDA_DISABLE_GRAPHS=1. Diagnosis: the banded rel-bias / shortconv path defeats llama-side graph reuse. This is measurement, not vendor restatement. (6) THE AUP WAS FETCHED (Stage 1 never did) AND IS NOT A BLOCKER. thinkingmachines.ai/model-acceptable-use-policy binds independently of Apache-2.0 ("By accessing, downloading, or using any Model Materials, you agree to be bound by this Model AUP") and is a separate document not incorporated by reference. Its prohibitions are behavioural only; there is NO restriction on commercial use, self-hosting, redistribution or fine-tuning, and no termination clause. Record the licence as "Apache-2.0 plus a binding behavioural AUP". (7) The page instructs users to `gh pr checkout 25731` and build from source - i.e. THE VENDOR ITSELF DOCUMENTS THAT MAINLINE llama.cpp DOES NOT SUPPORT THIS MODEL. (8) ROLE VERDICT: NOT a worker_general candidate, decisively. Against our roofline (gemma4 ~2.0 GB active-bytes/token -> 48.5 t/s CPU), Inkling-Small reads 3.58 GB/tok at UD-IQ2_M and 5.52 at UD-IQ4_XS, projecting to roughly 13-28 t/s - 2-4x SLOWER before novel-architecture overhead, with our own DeepSeek-V4 port as the calibrating precedent (active-param extrapolation predicted 18-23 t/s; actual was 9.13). worker_general is additionally aliased by worker_math and toolrunner and runs multi-instance, so a 16 GB -> 82-127 GB swap in the most replicated lane is categorically wrong. NOT an architect_critic candidate either: active bytes are at PARITY with the Qwen3.5-122B-A10B incumbent (5.52 vs 5.66 GB/tok), so there is no speed win, and adopting it would PERMANENTLY CLOSE that role's best upgrade path because the 122B has a measured, eval-parity-passed fully-GPU-resident IQ2 path at 43.7 t/s single / 148.7 aggregate on the MI210 while Inkling's smallest quant (82.4 GB) cannot fit 64 GB of VRAM.

### Verdict

worth_investigating, not new_opportunity and not not_applicable. Three gates in order of severity. (1) KERNEL: llama.cpp support is an un-merged draft PR adding a genuinely new architecture, against a FROZEN production-consolidated-v8 - the most expensive class of intake outcome, same as intake-279#record/281 (GLM-5 DSA) and intake-739#record (DeepSeek-V4-Pro). (2) The vendor headline 270 GB path for the flagship lands on UD-IQ1_S/M, and IQ1 remains STUBBED under GGML_IQK - a sharper repeat of the GLM-5.2 finding at intake-880#record/871. (3) STORAGE: /mnt/raid0 has 159 GB free of 3.7 TB (96% used), so the 270 GB flagship does not fit at all today. Still worth investigating because Inkling-Small at 276B/12B-active is a bandwidth-favourable CPU-decode shape, it is Apache-2.0 with no licence gate, and the right next step is cheap and inference-free: read PR #25731 to size the port rather than download weights.

### Linked handoffs and actions

- updated `handoffs/active/architect-model-selection-bench.md`
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] SETTLE THE GATE FIRST: read llama.cpp PR #25731 to size the port before any weight download. It is titled "Add TML Inkling architecture", authored by danielhanchen (the Unsloth founder, so not independent validation of arch maturity), and was in DRAFT state as of 2026-07-30.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Route the IQ1 collision to tq3-quantization-evaluation.md: the only sub-300 GB Inkling-975B quant is UD-IQ1_S/M and IQ1 is exactly the type GGML_IQK leaves stubbed. Quantify what an unaccelerated IQ1 costs before treating 270 GB as a viable target.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Storage gate precedes everything: /mnt/raid0 shows 159 GB free of 3.7 TB. Inkling-975B 1-bit (270 GB) does not fit; only Inkling-Small at 3-bit or smaller fits without a reclamation pass.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Scope any evaluation to Inkling-Small (276B/12B-active) first, not the 975B - it is the only member simultaneously storage-plausible and CPU-decode-favourable, and 12B active is directly comparable to our 122B-A10B architect incumbent.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Treat the claimed SWEBench-Verified 77.6% as unvalidated vendor-restated-creator self-report; architect-model-selection-bench.md own history with the enable_thinking and CoT-budget confounds is the reason to insist on in-harness re-measurement.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] The reasoning_effort continuous knob (0.00-0.99) is an independently interesting control-plane primitive for cost-aware routing even if the model is never deployed - it is finer-grained than our binary enable_thinking flag.

### Dive corrections

- 2026-08-03 DIVE-A. The quant enumeration and the architecture gate both hold; four corrections. (1) THE ARCHITECTURE GATE IS CONFIRMED FIRST-HAND. Read-only grep of the frozen tree at production-consolidated-v8 @ 67a433bf4 (branch and HEAD verified unchanged, zero tracked modifications): src/llama-arch.h contains exactly 138 LLM_ARCH_ entries ending at LLM_ARCH_DFLASH at line 149, with zero `inkling` matches anywhere. (2) STORAGE CORRECTION carried from the parent session: the Stage-1 agent's "storage is a non-issue ... against 3.7 TB raid0" reasoned from TOTAL capacity. df reports 158-159 GB AVAILABLE of 3.7 TB (96% used), and /mnt/raid0 is the same device as /. UD-Q4_K_M and UD-Q4_K_XL at ~163 GB DO NOT FIT. The IQ1 tier (UD-IQ1_S 74.8 GB, UD-IQ1_M 78.8 GB) is the only tier leaving real headroom, and IQ1 is STUBBED in our GGML_IQK build - so the quants that fit are unaccelerated and the quants that are accelerated barely fit. (3) SOURCING CORRECTION: the accuracy-retention table is NOT on this repo page. It lives on the Unsloth docs page (intake-941#record). Do not cite intake-942#record for it. (4) SOURCE DEFECT worth recording: this page advertises `llama serve -hf unsloth/Inkling-Small-GGUF:UD-Q4_K_M` WITH NO MENTION of the required unmerged PR, implying mainline support that does not exist. Treat the repo page as unreliable on support status; the docs page is correct. (5) SCOPE CLARIFICATION on the no-drafter finding: it is correct FOR THIS REPO (all 115 files enumerated, no draft/EAGLE/MTP artifact), but intake-955#record shows the BASE model config.json DOES carry mtp_config with 8 nextn predict layers and ships a separate mtp.safetensors. Both statements are true at their own scope. Note that the llama.cpp converter then STRIPS those tensors (see intake-941#record), so self-speculation is unavailable through the GGUF path regardless. (6) FAVOURABLE ARCHITECTURAL FACT Stage 1 missed: sliding_window_size 512 with local_layer_ids covering 35 of 42 layers - only SEVEN global attention layers. KV-cache cost is far lower than a 276B model implies. This is the one genuinely favourable finding for long-context roles and is a transferable signal for future candidates. (7) Vendor eval table CORRECTION: 77.6% and 80.2% are TWO DIFFERENT MODELS. Inkling-Small scores 80.2% on SWE-bench Verified; the 975B flagship scores 77.6%. Small BEATS the flagship. Methodology caveat to carry: "All evals run at effort 0.99 and temperature 1.0. All coding evals run with 256K max-token trajectory limit", no scaffold named, vendor self-report. (8) CONTEXT DISPUTED: config.json says model_max_length 1048576, but Artificial Analysis states 256K for Inkling-Small. Do not file 1M as settled. (9) THIRD-PARTY QUALITY EVIDENCE (genuinely independent, unlike the vendor pages): Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index 40 for Inkling-Small against 41 for the flagship, DeepSeek V4 Flash 40, MiniMax-M3 44 at 23B active and GLM-5.2 51 at 40B active; AA-Briefcase 917 Elo vs the flagship's 839; ~24K output tokens per task, notably terse.

### Verdict

worth_investigating. Not new_opportunity and emphatically not not_applicable - the gate is a verified missing architecture in the frozen tree, not a scope question. Adoption costs a full experimental-branch forward-port plus GPU/CPU regression validation plus v9 promotion, exactly the laguna-s21-cpu-port.md and deepseek-v4-flash-cpu-port.md precedent, and the repo publishes no version guidance to bound that cost. Favourable counterweights: IQ1-only is NOT the failure mode feared - the full IQ2/IQ3/IQ4_XS/K ladder that GGML_IQK already accelerates is offered, so the IQ1 stub can simply be avoided. Upgrade trigger: confirm the upstream arch PR merge status.

- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Confirm whether the inkling arch is MERGED into ggml-org/llama.cpp (PR #25731) or exists only on a personal fork branch. Merged upstream implies a scoped forward-port on the Laguna template; fork-only means park the entry. Nothing else should be spent before this resolves.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] If the gate clears, candidate arms are UD-IQ2_M (82.44 GB) as a CPU-resident architect candidate with UD-Q3_K_XL (119.55 GB) as the quant-control arm - NOT UD-Q4_K_M, which does not fit in 159 GB of free space.

(1) THE ENTRY'S OWN CONDITION FOR adopt_component IS MET - VERDICT SHOULD CHANGE FROM adopt_patterns TO adopt_component. internal/cli/scan_helpers.go:56-64 ships two first-class LOCAL providers: an `ollama` preset (baseURL http://localhost:11434, authRequired false, wireProvider "openai") and a generic `openai-compat` preset (authRequired false, no default base URL so --base-url is mandatory). wireProvider "openai" routes to baseURL + "/v1/chat/completions" (client.go:348-349) - byte-for-byte what llama-server exposes - and README.md:127,131-141 documents both. phase.go:36-38 states the intent: "BaseURL overrides the hardcoded per-provider default. Use for proxies, Azure OpenAI, Vertex-vs-AI-Studio, local mock servers." The entry's note that "NO local, open-weight or self-hosted model is named anywhere in the post" is accurate about the POST but must not be read as a property of the TOOL - this is a blog-versus-code gap. (2) Three things make it land rather than merely compile. PER-PHASE heterogeneity is free (phase.go:117-197 resolves provider, model, base-url and key independently per phase with an analysis->{feature-detection, audit} inheritance cascade), so analysis can run on a local GGUF and audit on a metered model or the reverse - that IS the cost-aware-routing ablation, already built. Unknown models degrade safely to 128K/8192 defaults with per-phase overrides. And the client SELF-HEALS against non-conforming servers (client.go:264-307 learns and latches forced tool_choice, temperature, and max_tokens->max_completion_tokens) - exactly llama-server's divergence class. Frictions, non-blocking: pricing assumes per-million rates so set --max-cost 0 or register a zero-price model; and tokenizerSafetyMargin = 0.20 (scan.go:496) is Anthropic-BPE-calibrated and is a guess for GGUF tokenizers. (3) THE CONFIDENCE THRESHOLD IS 0.3 (config.go:160; batch size 25; max-cost $25) AND IT IS NEARLY INERT. The prompt's own scale (audit.yaml:211-217) is REJECTED below 0.50 and CONFIRMED at or above 0.70, and applyAuditVerdicts drops on `verdict == "rejected" || confidence < threshold` (scan_audit.go:403). Because the model self-assigns the verdict token at its own 0.50 boundary, the numeric gate almost never bites. If we lift "their threshold" we would be lifting a no-op; lift the PROMPT BANDS instead. (4) THE AUDIT PROMPT CARRIES ITS OWN NEGATIVE RESULT, and it is the highest-value liftable artifact. audit.yaml:5-13 records the measurement that forced the rewrite: "rejected=0 across all runs. The audit added findings (42->47) and declared 'no false positives' while the annotation set disagreed on 14 of them. Two structural causes: 1. No null hypothesis. CONFIRMED was the default ... 2. 'Report any NEW findings' turned the filter into a second discovery pass." The fix is a four-gate ORDERING our eight gates do not encode: GATE 0 PRODUCTION REACHABILITY (:58-116) runs BEFORE the exploitability gates and SHORT-CIRCUITS, with an explicit null hypothesis ("the finding is non-production. You must DISPROVE this to proceed"); then reachability, absence-of-mitigation (which demands naming the location where the mitigation WOULD be), and material impact; plus a MANDATORY PRE-CONFIRM REFUTATION ("BEFORE issuing CONFIRMED ... write down the strongest case AGAINST it ... If you cannot refute it, you cannot confirm the finding"). (5) ANTI-ANCHORING IS IMPLEMENTED AT THE DATA LAYER, not only in prose (scan_audit.go:92-106): the field is renamed `unverified_exploit_sketch` and the payload wrapped as {"claims_to_verify": [...]}, with the prompt instructing that hedges are hypotheses to verify - "'The finding itself acknowledges it is not exploitable' is never a valid REJECTED reason on its own." This partially PRE-EMPTS the intake-875#record de-anchoring recommendation and de-anchors in BOTH directions. (6) AMEND THE intake-836#record CONTRADICTION. It remains CONFIRMED that no false-reject RATE is measured, but TWO STRUCTURAL MITIGATIONS EXIST: a `rejected` verdict with empty blocking_code is COERCED to `unverified` and the finding SURVIVES (scan_audit.go:377-385, rationale "multi-file invariants routinely get rejected here just because the auditor couldn't re-prove the whole chain in one pass"), and `unverified` findings are RETAINED with confidence floored at the threshold so a low score cannot re-drop them (:390-399) - a live instance of reviewer-decision-plane.md's three-valued outcome. Unmeasured is not unmitigated; our adoption must add the measurement, and intake-948#record is the instrument for it. (7) CORRECT the prompt-set count: FOURTEEN sets ship, not three - default, carlini, carlini-curated, exploit-proof plus seven language variants, and nano-analyzer. cwe_deep_analysis.yaml is 58 KB covering 30 CWEs on a uniform {title, analysis_prompt, validation_checks[], false_positive_indicators[]} schema spliced per finding at audit time, whose header records the calibration lesson that the previous FP indicators "were too exotic to fire ... while the actual FPs were mundane". (8) DEDUP: the (fileURI, startLine, CWE) highest-severity-wins keying and its ordering BEFORE the LLM audit are both CONFIRMED (postprocess.go:63-98; scan.go:784 -> 787 -> 815), but the key is anchored on startLine EQUALITY with no range overlap and no CWE-equivalence, so findings at 88-166 and 90-166 with the same CWE both survive. LIFT THE ORDERING FROM HERE AND THE KEY FROM intake-948#record's matcher.

### Verdict

adopt_patterns, not adopt_component, because the shipped artifact is a Go SAST CLI aimed at hosted frontier models through a databricks provider and we are not in the SAST product business. IF the provider layer accepts an OpenAI-compatible base URL - unverified from the post - adopt_component becomes live on two fronts at once: a self-hosted SAST pass over our own repos, and a ready-made realistic long-context cross-file agent workload for reviewer-model ablations. Not already_integrated (four mechanisms are new); not superseded (nothing in the index covers the compaction/budget/SARIF-determinism half); not not_applicable (in-scope and open source).

## intake-944#record — QuixiCore - umbrella kernel-contract and conformance-registry repo for six native AI kernel backends

- **URL**: https://github.com/QuixiAI/QuixiCore
- **Verification**: dive-verified
- **Verdict**: worth_investigating (novelty medium, relevance high; categories: hardware_optimization, quantization, benchmark_methodology, local_inference, agent_architecture)

### Claims (as verified)

0. It is NOT an agent framework, orchestrator or inference engine - it is a specification and registry repo: native implementations, shared contract, no shared code.
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-I, operator-scoped to how others approach experimental kernel work on the AMD/NVIDIA gap, with intake-947#record folded in. verdict worth_investigating STANDS.
1. The umbrella deliberately holds no implementation: backend registry metadata, kernel and quantization format registries, correctness specifications and architecture support matrices, but no backend implementation code or build systems.
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-I, operator-scoped to how others approach experimental kernel work on the AMD/NVIDIA gap, with intake-947#record folded in. verdict worth_investigating STANDS.
   - anchor `matched span in https://github.com/QuixiAI/QuixiCore`: "It contains: Backend registry metadata Kernel and quant format registries Correctness and benchmarking specifications Coverage and architecture support matrices Release and naming policy Test-vector organization It intentionally does not contain backend implementation code, shared kernel code, pl..."
2. The ROCm backend is entirely unfinished and CDNA3-scoped - its status records operation-level CDNA3 progress, not CDNA2-through-CDNA4 coverage.
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-I, operator-scoped to how others approach experimental kernel work on the AMD/NVIDIA gap, with intake-947#record folded in. verdict worth_investigating STANDS.
3. The CPU backend claims semantic-contract completeness over 127 operations validated on Apple AArch64 and Intel Sapphire Rapids - notably NOT AMD Zen5.
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-I, operator-scoped to how others approach experimental kernel work on the AMD/NVIDIA gap, with intake-947#record folded in. verdict worth_investigating STANDS.
4. GGUF is a first-class read target, matching ggml (not llama.cpp) as the reference semantics.
   - *narrowed*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-I, operator-scoped to how others approach experimental kernel work on the AMD/NVIDIA gap, with intake-947#record folded in. verdict worth_investigating STANDS. One of the two GGUF claims DOWNGRADED TO PARTIAL, verified read-only against our own tree. Treat GGUF-as-reference-semantics as partial rather than established.
5. Documented GGUF/MX divergence: E8M0 code 255 yields +Inf in GGUF versus NaN in MX formats, because ggml reconstructs the scale by bit-punning the code into the fp32 exponent field with no special cases.
   - *narrowed*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-I, operator-scoped to how others approach experimental kernel work on the AMD/NVIDIA gap, with intake-947#record folded in. verdict worth_investigating STANDS. This is the sharper downgrade and it is wrong in a way that matters. 'E8M0 code 255 yields +Inf in GGUF' is FALSE TWICE: ggml-impl.h:439-473 special-cases code 0, and the 255->NaN branch is present-but-COMMENTED with the rationale 'disabled as we don't need to handle NaNs'. Worse, GGML_E8M0_TO_FP32 HAS ZERO CALL SITES — every CPU MXFP4 path uses ggml_e8m0_to_fp32_half, where (255-1)<<23 = 0x7F000000 = 2^127, which is FINITE, neither +Inf nor NaN. So the documented divergence does not describe the code that actually runs.
6. IQ2_XXS decode cost is characterized as four dependent random reads of a 256-entry grid plus a sign-table read.
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-I, operator-scoped to how others approach experimental kernel work on the AMD/NVIDIA gap, with intake-947#record folded in. verdict worth_investigating STANDS.
7. AGENTS.md imposes a measurement gate on AI contributors: no performance claim may be committed without a focused optimization run logging hardware, driver/runtime, command line, git commit, warmups, iterations, median, variance and an explicit keep/reject decision.
   - *unaffected*: 2026-08-03 DIVE-I, operator-scoped to how others approach experimental kernel work on the AMD/NVIDIA gap, with intake-947#record folded in. verdict worth_investigating STANDS.

### Dive corrections

- 2026-08-03 DIVE-I, scoped by the operator to "how others are thinking about experimental kernel work to close the AMD/NVIDIA gap", with intake-947#record folded in. verdict worth_investigating STANDS. (1) BOTH GGUF CLAIMS DOWNGRADED TO PARTIAL, verified read-only against our own tree. E8M0: the bit-punning mechanism is confirmed, but "with no special cases" is FALSE TWICE - ggml-impl.h:439-473 special-cases code 0, and the 255->NaN branch is present-but-commented with the stated rationale "disabled as we don't need to handle NaNs". Worse, GGML_E8M0_TO_FP32 HAS ZERO CALL SITES: every CPU MXFP4 path uses ggml_e8m0_to_fp32_half (:477), where (255-1)<<23 = 0x7F000000 = 2^127, FINITE - neither +Inf nor NaN. And ggml DOES special-case 255, at LOAD VALIDATION: validate_e_e8m0() (ggml-quants.c:5366-5372) rejects e==0xff as invalid row data, wired for MXFP4 at :5560 and called from llama-model-loader.cpp, so a GGUF carrying code 255 is REFUSED - treating 0xFF as reserved exactly as OCP MX intends. The claim also omits a THREE-WAY BACKEND DIVERGENCE: CPU yields finite 2^127, Metal/SYCL/Vulkan/OpenCL/HIP yield +Inf, CUDA >= 12.8 yields NaN; on our MI210 HIP build CUDART_VERSION is undefined so we take the +Inf fallback (ggml-cuda/common.cuh:816-822). IQ2_XXS: "four" and "256-entry grid" are confirmed (GGML_TABLE_BEGIN(uint64_t, iq2xxs_grid, 256), 2 KiB, L1-resident), but "DEPENDENT" is OVERTURNED - all four indices arrive in a SINGLE 8-byte memcpy (ggml-quants.c:2500) so no address depends on another lookup's result, giving four INDEPENDENT data-dependent gathers at ILP ~ 4 rather than a pointer chase; and "a sign-table read" UNDERCOUNTS 4x because ksigns_iq2xs[] is inside the l loop. The ratio 4 grid + 4 sign holds across AVX2, CUDA and our production iqk path. CORRECT STATEMENT: per 32 quantized weights, four independent data-dependent gathers into a 256-entry / 2 KiB grid plus four sign-table gathers, each grid entry expanding to 8 values. (2) THE NUMBER ATTACHED TO THE IQ2 CLAIM BREAKS QUIXICORE'S OWN GATE. The spec's "measured on CDNA3 that is 1.07x ... against 3.4x for decode-at-load" traces to a ROCm kernel README that actually says "Measured in vLLM at DeepSeek-V4 expert shapes (4096 -> 2048, 32 experts, top-6) ... at 512 routed tokens". One hop later the umbrella spec drops vLLM, drops the shapes, drops the token count, re-attributes it to "CDNA3", and generalises it to "a property of the format, not of any one backend". The commit that produced it (73e40a641b10, 2026-08-02) touched five files and NOT perf/optimization_status.md, which their own AGENTS.md mandates for any performance claim. Their contract is well-designed and their most recent kernel commit bypassed it - an unenforced measurement contract is decoration. (3) cdna2 IS DECLARED IN FOUR MANIFESTS AND IMPLEMENTED IN ZERO FILES: 0 hits for gfx90a, cdna2 or mi210 across 1251 blobs in QuixiCore-ROCm, against 469 cdna3 and 272 cdna4. (4) QuixiCore-ROCm is CONFIRMED a hard fork of HazyResearch/HipKittens (GitHub API parent/source), MIT. QuixiCore-CPU has NO LICENCE, confirmed three ways (metadata null, /license 404, no root LICENSE entry) - the only repo in the family without one. (5) The AGENTS.md gate is CONFIRMED verbatim, but ADD that it carries NO NUMERIC THRESHOLD at the umbrella level (thresholds live in the per-backend perf.md at >=3% low-risk / >=8-10% with added complexity), NO anti-fabrication clause, and NO revert rule. WHAT IT HAS THAT WE DO NOT, and is worth importing: a CAPABILITY-CLAIM gate ("do not claim a backend supports a kernel, dtype, quant format or performance tier unless the corresponding backend repo has correctness AND performance evidence"); a MECHANISM-PLAUSIBILITY requirement ("'It got faster and I don't know why' is a reason to keep measuring, not to land"), which is absent from our purely statistical P-AK-SEARCH-1; a first-class rejection ledger; and TEST-VECTOR-AS-CONTRACT, which "caught four backends decoding the same E8M0 byte three different ways at the edges. Nothing had compared them because nothing ran" - and this dive found exactly that defect class LIVE in our tree. (6) FREE INCIDENTAL FINDING IN OUR OWN TREE, unrelated to either claim: iqk_gemm_iquants.cpp contains an AVX512-VPOPCNTDQ routine deriving IQ2 signs arithmetically with ZERO table reads, but its dispatch at :184 is guarded by `#if defined z_HAVE_FANCY_SIMD && ...`. The z_ prefix makes the macro undefinable, so the branch is PERMANENTLY DEAD (same at :109 and :571) and the table lookup always runs - including on our Zen 5 host, which HAS VPOPCNTDQ. Inherited verbatim from ik_llama.cpp via fec061dea; not introduced by us. Any follow-up belongs on an experimental branch, never the frozen tree. STAGE-2b ADDENDUM 2026-08-03 (dive 2b-A on the QuixiAI org, dive 2b-I on the conformance artifacts). THE CONFORMANCE VECTORS ARE THE CHEAPEST HIGH-VALUE ARTIFACT IN THE WHOLE DIVE - MIT, ~8 KB, four JSON files (e8m0.json, e8m0_gguf.json, mxfp4.json, e2m1.json, 815 B - 5.1 KB). Scalar structure {format, spec, note, cases[]}, each case {code, value, bits} - THE DECODED FLOAT AND ITS EXACT HEX BIT PATTERN, so a decoder is pinned BIT-EXACTLY rather than to a tolerance. Coverage is deliberately edge-weighted: codes 0, 1, 2, 126, 127, 128, 253, 254, 255. THE SINGLE MOST TRANSFERABLE DESIGN DECISION: the same format appears TWICE AS TWO SEPARATE CONTRACTS - e8m0.json (MX spec, 255 -> NaN) and e8m0_gguf.json (ggml behaviour, 255 -> +Inf, bits 0x7f800000) - with the note "Kept as a separate contract so a backend cannot satisfy one by breaking the other." That is precisely the discipline our tree lacks, and it is the mechanism that would have caught the three different answers for code 255 across seven backend sites this dive found live in our own tree, WHILE KEEPING the ggml-compat path legitimate rather than declaring it a bug. AN ANTI-REWARD-HACKING ARGUMENT INSIDE A TEST FIXTURE, which maps onto our C6 differentiator: mxfp4.json pins block positions with the note that "swapping the halves leaves the value multiset and the block norm almost unchanged, so a norm-only check passes a wrong decoder - these vectors pin positions." THE COMPANION MATRIX DISTINGUISHES VERIFIED FROM ASSERTED PER ROW - every row carries a `Checked by` column naming the test file, or `not yet checked`: "A backend is conformant only if a test in that repo consumes those vectors; anything else is an observation from reading the source and is marked as such." It records legitimate divergence as documented-divergent rather than failing, and it SELF-INDICTS: "Until then it is hand-written and will drift - the same failure mode it exists to document." GOVERNANCE, UNCHANGED: cross-backend conformance vectors are an OPERATOR DECISION ITEM, not a task line. They are a NEW INSTRUMENT, and instruments touch the measurement trust boundary. THE PERF LEDGER (QuixiCore-ROCm, now intake-959#record) supplies the two gaps this dive identified: a Sec 7 "Rejected - with the reason, so they are not retried" section with dated entries and mechanisms, and a Sec 12 decision rule with a MECHANISM-PLAUSIBILITY clause - "There is a plausible explanation backed by bytes, FLOPs, counters, or a clean A/B. 'It got faster and I don't know why' is a reason to keep measuring, not to land." Reading them in full CONFIRMS rather than expands the earlier verdict. TWO HONEST CAVEATS: (a) their thresholds are FIXED LITERALS (3% / 8-10%) with NO STATISTICAL TEST AT ALL - median and p20/p80 - materially WEAKER than P-AK-SEARCH-1's anytime-valid e-processes and published MDE. ADOPT THE SHAPE, NOT THE NUMBERS. (b) their Sec 5 timing recipe assumes rocprofv3, which is NOT INSTALLED on this host. ORG CONTEXT NOW ESTABLISHED (2b-A): the org's llama.cpp fork is a zero-delta mirror (intake-957#record) and QuixiFlow's main is likewise zero-delta with three stale branches (intake-958#record). The real work is in QuixiCore-ROCm (intake-959#record), which declares cdna2 in four manifests and implements it in ZERO FILES.

### Verdict

In-domain and MI210-adjacent, but nothing is usable today: the ROCm backend is in progress across all 16 kernel families and is CDNA3-scoped, and the CPU backend has no licence. Both of this entry's GGUF claims are downgraded to PARTIAL — the 'no special cases' claim is false twice against our own tree.
worth_investigating. In-domain and MI210-adjacent so not_applicable is wrong. Not adopt_component: the ROCm backend is in-progress on every one of 16 kernel families, is explicitly CDNA3-scoped, and the CPU backend has no licence - none of it is usable today. Not superseded: nothing in the index occupies this slot. The investigable value is threefold and none of it requires adopting QuixiCore itself - it surfaced HipKittens; its GGUF spec contains two locally checkable claims; and its AGENTS.md performance gate is an independent convergent design on MEASUREMENT.md worth reading as external corroboration.

- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Feed the IQ2_XXS decode-cost characterization (four dependent random reads of a 256-entry grid plus a sign-table read) into mi210-q8-dequant-gemv-roofline.md as a mechanism-level explanation complementing the measured Q4_K/Q8 roofline gaps.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [unverified] Read AGENTS.md performance gate as external corroboration for MEASUREMENT.md when reviewing the agentic-rocm-kernel-authoring loop - an independent project converged on baseline+candidate, warmups, iterations, median, variance, git commit and an explicit keep/reject record. Cite as convergent practice; adopt nothing verbatim.
