0. main is zero-delta: ahead_by 0, behind_by 329, merge_base equals its own HEAD. All content lives in three branches, all 6518 commits behind main, all merge-basing at cc410e864480 (2026-01-02), never merged even to the fork own main.

not_applicable. 0. A 77-commits-ahead hard fork of HazyResearch/HipKittens, 300 files, containing real measured kernel work with quantified per-kernel gains.
1. THE METHODOLOGICALLY INTERESTING PART: the commit log records REJECTED experiments with their mechanism - "reject LDS-B experiment (inconsistent across shapes)", "reject (CU contention)". That is a working accept/reject discipline in the wild, close in shape to our own measurement constitution.
2. CDNA2 SUPPORT IS DECLARED AND NOT IMPLEMENTED. .quixicore/backend.yaml declares targets [cdna2, cdna3, cdna4], but the 1759-path tree has ZERO paths matching cdna2/gfx90a/gfx908 (cdna3: 583, cdna4: 347). Its own README states flatly "We support CDNA3 and CDNA 4."
3. All measurements are MI300X gfx942 / ROCm 7.2.4 - neither our architecture nor our ROCm version.
4. Its perf ledger separates Wins from "Rejected - with the reason, so they are not retried", each entry DATED "so it can be challenged with new data", framed as "current truth until re-measured".

### Verdict

worth_investigating, not adopt_component: no code is portable to gfx90a (zero cdna2 paths despite the manifest declaration). Not not_applicable: the accept/reject discipline is directly transferable and the q8_0-to-q5_K rejection is a mechanism finding that bears on our own quant-ladder program. The upstream it forks is peer-reviewed (MLSys 2026) and landed in AITER, so this is a fork of independently validated work rather than a self-report.

## intake-964#record — llvm-project AMDGPU TableGen as the authoritative gfx90a capability oracle (BuiltinsAMDGPU.td / GCNProcessors.td / AMDGPU.td)

- **URL**: https://github.com/llvm/llvm-project
- **Verification**: dive-verified
- **Verdict**: adopt_component (novelty high, relevance high; categories: hardware_optimization)
- **Expanded from**: intake-947#record

### Claims (as verified)

0. A four-step, end-to-end-verified recipe answers any "does gfx90a have instruction X" question authoritatively and offline, in seconds, with no GPU.
1. CORRECTION THAT MUST BE CARRIED: AMDGPU.td does NOT contain the processor definitions. def GFX90A lives in GCNProcessors.td (line 171), which AMDGPU.td includes. A future session grepping only AMDGPU.td for gfx90a will get a MISLEADING PARTIAL ANSWER.
2. Feature sets resolve through !listconcat chains, so the parent MUST be followed or the answer is a FALSE NEGATIVE. gfx90a: FeatureISAVersion9_0_A (1904) -> FeatureISAVersion9_0_MI_Common (1850) -> FeatureISAVersion9_0_Common -> FeatureGFX9.
3. THE FINAL QUOTED STRING IN BuiltinsAMDGPU.td IS THE GATE. Reading rule: gated mai-insts or gfx90a-insts => AVAILABLE on gfx90a; gated fp8-insts, gfx940-insts, gfx950-insts, gfx12-insts, gfx1250-insts or any *-cvt-scale-insts => NOT.
4. CAVEAT THAT MUST RIDE WITH THE RECIPE: this oracle answers ISA CAPABILITY ONLY. It says nothing about whether HIPCC will GENERATE GOOD CODE - the HipKittens AGPR/MFMA finding is exactly a case where the ISA permits what the compiler will not emit. Capability != codegen quality != performance. Three distinct questions; LLVM answers only the first.

### Verdict

adopt_component: the recipe is the artifact and it is directly usable. Pin at an LLVM RELEASE TAG, not main, so answers are reproducible.

- updated `handoffs/active/mi210-q8-dequant-gemv-roofline.md`
- updated `handoffs/active/rocm-verify-profile-backend.md`
- updated `handoffs/active/gpu-acceleration-path.md`
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] ADOPT the four-step recipe as the standing reference procedure for gfx90a capability questions, pinned at an LLVM release tag.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] CARRY the AMDGPU.td-does-not-hold-processors correction prominently - it is the specific way a future session gets a wrong answer.

## intake-965#record — apache/tvm - where the ROCm codegen actually lives, and the only compile target open to gfx90a by construction

- **URL**: https://github.com/apache/tvm
- **Verification**: dive-verified
- **Verdict**: worth_investigating (novelty high, relevance high; categories: hardware_optimization, inference_serving)
- **Expanded from**: intake-500#record

### Claims (as verified)

0. INTAKE-500 POINTS AT THE WRONG REPO for the ROCm question. ROCm codegen lives in apache/tvm, NOT mlc-llm - mlc-llm tree contains ZERO rocm/hip/gfx paths.
1. THE DECISIVE MECHANISM: TVM python/tvm/support/rocm.py registers tvm_callback_rocm_get_arch, which shells out to rocminfo and regex-matches Name:\s+(gfx\d+[a-zA-Z]*), falling back to gfx900. THERE IS NO ALLOWLIST AND NO gfx942 GATE. On our box it resolves gfx90a automatically and hands it to LLVM AMDGPU backend.
2. This FALSIFIES any "MLC/TVM is CDNA3-scoped" assumption. Unlike TileLang, HipKittens and AITER, it genuinely is not.
3. WHAT IS THIN, AND IT BOUNDS THE AMBITION: TVM tensor_intrin/rocm.py registers only THREE MFMA compute intrinsics - f32f32f32 (k=4), f16f16f32 (k=16), s8s8s32 (k=16), all 16x16. THERE IS NO bf16 MFMA INTRINSIC REGISTERED, and bf16 is exactly the datatype where we already have parity capability. Adding one is plausible (names are built from a template) but it is WORK, not adoption.

### Verdict

worth_investigating, deliberately not adopt_component: architectural openness is established, competitive performance on gfx90a is not, and the missing bf16 intrinsic sits exactly on our parity datatype. The honest framing is that its value is NOT that it will beat our hand-tuned path.

- updated `handoffs/active/mi210-q8-dequant-gemv-roofline.md`
- updated `handoffs/active/rocm-verify-profile-backend.md`
- updated `handoffs/active/gpu-acceleration-path.md`

- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] REGISTER MLC/TVM as a school-4 backend option in the AutoKernel option set, with the correct mechanism and the bf16-intrinsic caveat attached.

Precondition for upgrade narrows: avoid ROCm 7.2.0 specifically; 7.2.1+ reverted it. Separate newer risk: LLVM 23 #194924 enables runtime unrolling for all AMDGPU loops (therock-10.1 gates it off).

## intake-970#record — How Efficient is LLM-Generated Code? A Rigorous and High-Standard Benchmark (ENAMEL)

- **URL**: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06647
- **Verification**: dive-verified
- **Verdict**: adopt_patterns (novelty high, relevance high; categories: benchmark_methodology)
- **Expanded from**: intake-953#record

### Claims (as verified)

0. RIGHT-CENSORING IS THE TRANSFERABLE PRIMITIVE, and it is a primitive we hold nowhere. Per-level score f = (T_i - max_m t)^+ / (T_i - max_m t*), worst-case over the M_l cases in a level: once max_m t >= T_i the clamp makes f = 0 "regardless of the exact value". The unknowable censored magnitude is DISCHARGED rather than IMPUTED.
1. Generalized: make the score dependence on the measurement VANISH at exactly the point the measurement becomes uninformative.
2. eff@k is an unbiased estimator with Var <= (k/n) Var[naive] (Theorem 1), computed over ascending order statistics.
3. R=6 repeats aggregated by the HODGES-LEHMANN estimator (median of pairwise means) for "robustness against outliers as well as high statistical efficiency".
4. ENAMEL IS NOT THE SOURCE OF THE DISPUTED NORMALIZATION CLAIM. grep -ci "qiu|enamel|eff@k" over SWE-fficiency v3 returns 0, 0, 0 - it never cites ENAMEL. NOT-FOUND-IN-SOURCE.

### Verdict

Adopt the metric, the censoring rule and the Hodges-Lehmann aggregator as a drop-in. Do NOT expect to run the harness as shipped on this host.
adopt_patterns, not adopt_component: the metric and the censoring rule transfer; the harness cannot be run as shipped on this host. The Hodges-Lehmann aggregator is separately a drop-in.

### Linked handoffs and actions

- updated `handoffs/active/architect-model-selection-bench.md`
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] PREPARE right-censoring as a MEASUREMENT-CONSTITUTION decision package - human-only boundary, do not self-apply. Score -> 0 at the cap; never impute the censored value.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] BLOCKING PRECONDITION on the above: if eff@k is adopted, run it under OUR OWN container isolation.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] ADOPT the Hodges-Lehmann estimator for aggregating R repeated timings - a drop-in with no constitutional implication.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] CARRY the saturation caveat: eff@k cannot distinguish a 2x from a 1000x. Do not use it where magnitude matters.

## intake-971#record — Evaluating Language Models for Efficient Code Generation (EvalPerf / Differential Performance Evaluation)

- **URL**: https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.06450
- **Verification**: dive-overturned
- **Verdict**: adopt_component (novelty high, relevance high; categories: benchmark_methodology)
- **Expanded from**: intake-953#record

### Claims (as verified)

0. THE OVERTURN: this - not ENAMEL - is the actual citation behind SWE-fficiency claim that normalized metrics inherently reduce variability, AND THE SOURCE ARGUES THE OPPOSITE.
   - *uncertain*: dive_corrections is empty. Only external corroboration exists (intake-953#record's closure resolves the citation to arXiv 2408.06450 = EvalPerf), not a correction recorded on this entry.
1. Sec A.2 verbatim in substance: relative speedup applicability "becomes less clear across a broad range of tasks. This is because the degree of speedup varies significantly across different tasks ... This variability can lead to confusion." EvalPerf introduces DPS BECAUSE relative speedup is variable.
   - *uncertain*: dive_corrections is empty. intake-953#record repeats this Sec A.2 quote approvingly, but no correction text exists here to establish any effect.
2. Its stability comes from HARDWARE PERFORMANCE COUNTERS and from SELECTING LARGE COMPUTATIONS, not from normalization.
   - *uncertain*: dive_corrections is empty. intake-953#record states the same mechanism (hardware performance counters, selecting large computations), but this entry carries no correction.
3. DPS adaptively CLUSTERS reference solutions by efficiency, cutting where delta > bias + sqrt(w/t_bar) - a noise-aware threshold that WIDENS FOR SHORTER RUNTIMES, motivated by their own measurement that CV rises as runtime falls.
   - *uncertain*: dive_corrections is empty. No prose anywhere discusses DPS clustering or the delta > bias + sqrt(w/t_bar) threshold, so no verdict is recoverable.
4. DPS is GRADED but ORDINAL: the score is where you land in the empirical distribution, so the magnitude-to-score mapping is set by that distribution SHAPE, not by physics; resolution is capped by m >= 4 clusters.
   - *uncertain*: dive_corrections is empty. No prose discusses DPS ordinality or the m >= 4 cluster-resolution cap, so no verdict is recoverable.

### Verdict

## intake-973#record — GSO: Challenging Software Optimization Tasks for Evaluating SWE-Agents

- **URL**: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23671
- **Verification**: dive-verified
- **Verdict**: adopt_patterns (novelty high, relevance high; categories: benchmark_methodology, agent_architecture)
- **Expanded from**: intake-953#record

### Claims (as verified)

0. IT CONTAINS OUR OWN STACK: 2 of its 102 tasks are abetlen/llama-cpp-python optimization tasks with EXPERT-COMMIT performance targets. That is the most on-domain evaluation asset in this entire cluster.
1. AGGREGATION IS THE SINGLE MOST TRANSFERABLE FINDING. Harmonic mean over per-test speedups, chosen explicitly over geometric mean citing Jacob & Mudge 1995: "A model achieving speedups of [0.1, 1000] across two tests yields a geometric mean of 10, despite degrading performance on one test. In Section 5, we show that agents INDEED PERFORM SUCH OPTIMIZATIONS and thus can game the geometric mean."
2. Appendix E also rejects the arithmetic mean and risk-adjusted geomean, because "we do not want symmetric treatment - large wins on minor tests should not hurt, only significant regressions matter." Harmonic mean asymmetric sensitivity "punishes slowdowns heavily, while almost ignoring large speedups."
3. Oracle-equivalence is a GOLDEN-FILE CHARACTERIZATION TEST, not a test suite. Six-function contract: setup() [UNTIMED] / experiment(data) [THE TIMED REGION] / store_result / load_result / check_equivalence(reference, current) / run_test(eqcheck, reference, prefix) -> float. The oracle is a stored JSON produced by running the same script against the PRE-optimization codebase.
4. Opt@K = Opt_{0.95}@K - 95% of the HUMAN EXPERT speedup, measured in the same container. Leading SWE-agents score under 5%.

### Verdict

adopt_patterns rather than adopt_component because the full 168 GB image set does not fit our 158 GB free. The AGGREGATION rule and the EQUIVALENCE-CONTRACT discipline are adopted; the timing discipline is explicitly declined; and the llama-cpp-python subset is separately affordable.

### Linked handoffs and actions

- updated `handoffs/active/architect-model-selection-bench.md`
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] ADOPT THE HARMONIC MEAN for aggregating per-test speedups and retire the geometric mean wherever we use it. HIGHEST-VALUE SINGLE FINDING in this cluster - GSO demonstrates agents GAME geomean. Audit current aggregation sites first.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] PULL the 2 llama-cpp-python tasks (~21 GB) and run them against our coder and architect roles - on-domain, MIT, expert-commit targets.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] IMPORT the six-function equivalence contract, but NOT the reduced-digest check and NOT number=1 timing.

### Dive corrections

- 2026-10-06 IRV-5 source re-verification: The source verifies a staged correctness-gated reward, a pre-correctness failure ladder, and normalized gap closure. It does not test whether correctness gating beats blending. Reported results are one-seed point estimates and do not establish component necessity. Official code has a 2% RBR tolerance absent from paper Eq.2, but no paper-run revision linkage proves the published table used that code. Local use is a candidate design, not an EPYC result. 2026-10-06 IRV native-slot mapping audit: Replace cross-entry independence warrant with separate-study/related architectural evidence; preserve unread939 scope. Original authoritative native key_claims (unchanged historical text): ["THE INDEPENDENCE TEST RESULT IS PARTIAL, AND THE KIND MATTERS: the corroboration for intake-939#record is ARCHITECTURAL, NOT EMPIRICAL. RLPF is a genuinely independent group that independently arrived at a correctness-gated staged reward, with released code implementing a strictly separated outer gate.", "BUT EVERY REWARD ARM RLPF TESTED IS CORRECTNESS-GATED. There is NO blended arm and NO ungated arm anywhere in the paper or the code. RLPF therefore CANNOT show that gating beats blending - it never ran the comparison. Its ablations test WHICH GATED TERMS matter, not WHETHER THE GATE matters.", "RLPF DESIGN CONTRADICTS intake-939#record WINNING ARM. 939 winner was the COLLAPSED CONJUNCTION (correct-but-slow -> full failure reward). RLPF explicitly and deliberately does the OPPOSITE: epsilon = 0.05 exists SOLELY to guarantee every correct program outranks every failed one. Two capable groups, OPPOSITE CHOICES within the same family.", "THE PRE-CORRECTNESS PROGRESS LADDER IS THE HIGHEST-VALUE TRANSFER: s(NX)=-0.05 < s(NC)=0.00 < s(NR)=+0.05 < s(WO)=+0.10. Our kernel loop currently treats ALL non-verifying candidates as EQUAL failures; ranking hipcc-fail < runtime-fail < wrong-output gives a SEARCH GRADIENT in the regime where most MI210 candidates die. intake-939#record has no analogue.", "CGRE supplies the CONTINUOUS normalized gap-closure form a hill-climbing search actually needs: (T_base - T_cand)/(T_base - T_ref), clipped to [0,1]."]. Original historical true refutations and dependencies remain preserved; narrowed current effects apply only to bounded replacement statements, never historical fabricated assertions.

### Verdict

The source verifies a staged correctness-gated reward, a pre-correctness failure ladder, and normalized gap closure. It does not test whether correctness gating beats blending. Reported results are one-seed point estimates and do not establish component necessity. Official code has a 2% RBR tolerance absent from paper Eq.2, but no paper-run revision linkage proves the published table used that code. Local use is a candidate design, not an EPYC result.
adopt_patterns, with adopt_component explicitly BLOCKED by the missing licence file and the absent benchmark tasks.

### Dive corrections

- 2026-10-06 IRV-5 source re-verification: The paper-linked public repository is now available, overturning the old anonymous-review-only/unreleased claim. No umbrella license is present, so availability is not permission to reuse. CUDA tooling is NVIDIA-oriented in the reviewed route and no HIP/ROCm route was found there. The 128 paper count and 125 pinned-tree count differ; do not use the old 5.2% test-split claim without version-specific evidence. Author overlap means no independent RLPF corroboration. Preserve later license/task-level review. 2026-10-06 IRV native-slot mapping audit: Retain four native identities; add accepted baseline/reference span toslot1 and accepted systems-content span to previously unanchoredslot2. Original authoritative native key_claims (unchanged historical text): ["TRACK, DO NOT ADOPT - and the reason is specific rather than general.", "ATTRACTIVE: it is the ONLY member of the cluster with genuine SYSTEMS content (OpenMP atomics, SIMD string processing, bitmap filtering, packed integer decoding, CUDA reductions/scans/tiled transpose) and the only one supplying BOTH a baseline AND an expert-optimized reference per task - which is what makes a normalized gap-closure metric possible at all.", "It is materially closer to MI210 kernel work than LeetCode-style tasks.", "FAILS THE BAR TODAY on four counts: not independent (same group as intake-974#record); NOT RELEASED (only an anonymous 4open.science review link, no licence); CUDA is 5.2% of the test split (16/306); and the CUDA path is NVIDIA-only via _detect_nvcc() with no ROCm/HIP path."]. Original historical true refutations and dependencies remain preserved; narrowed current effects apply only to bounded replacement statements, never historical fabricated assertions.

### Verdict

The paper-linked public repository is now available, overturning the old anonymous-review-only/unreleased claim. No umbrella license is present, so availability is not permission to reuse. CUDA tooling is NVIDIA-oriented in the reviewed route and no HIP/ROCm route was found there. The 128 paper count and 125 pinned-tree count differ; do not use the old 5.2% test-split claim without version-specific evidence. Author overlap means no independent RLPF corroboration. Preserve later license/task-level review.
worth_investigating with an explicit DEFER: re-check in roughly 60 days for a release, a licence, and any ROCm/HIP path. Not not_applicable - the design is right; the availability is not.

### Dive corrections

- 2026-10-06 IRV-5 source re-verification: The paper independently shows that only 6.11% of its four function-level Python benchmark comparisons were significant on original tests after 30 repeats. This supports a related test-sensitivity concern but does not measure duration spread or validate intake-939#record’s CV threshold/3.8% estimate. The 209/308 are plausible author-labeled improvements, not verified speedups. Keep the exact Mercury dependency because it supplies 256 denominator tasks; do not conflate Mercury’s corpus role with any separately named successor. 2026-10-06 IRV native-slot mapping audit: Restore original native identities rather than shifting accepted expanded assessment numbers into native indices; remove independence warrant. Original authoritative native key_claims (unchanged historical text): ["THE STRONGEST CORROBORATION THIS BATCH PRODUCED, and it is for a claim we were not testing. intake-939#record duration-spread finding - that robust CV >= 0.3 is satisfied for at most 3.8% of problems with ORIGINAL tests versus 48.2% with purpose-built ones - now has GENUINE INDEPENDENT EMPIRICAL CORROBORATION by a completely different route.", "Different group, different method: 30 repetitions per task plus statistical testing across EffiBench, ENAMEL, EvalPerf and Mercury; 1,538 tasks.", "Same conclusion: only 6.11% of purportedly performant reference implementations show a STATISTICALLY SIGNIFICANT improvement on the ORIGINAL tests.", "209 of 308 manually-analysed non-significant cases contain REAL improvements that the ORIGINAL TESTS FAIL TO EXPOSE - so the problem is the test workload, not the optimization.", "Their generated performance-oriented tests raise detection to 24-25%."]. Original historical true refutations and dependencies remain preserved; narrowed current effects apply only to bounded replacement statements, never historical fabricated assertions.

### Verdict

The paper independently shows that only 6.11% of its four function-level Python benchmark comparisons were significant on original tests after 30 repeats. This supports a related test-sensitivity concern but does not measure duration spread or validate intake-939#record’s CV threshold/3.8% estimate. The 209/308 are plausible author-labeled improvements, not verified speedups. Keep the exact Mercury dependency because it supplies 256 denominator tasks; do not conflate Mercury’s corpus role with any separately named successor.
adopt_patterns. Its finding is what we consume, not its harness.

Original correctness tests clear the duration-spread gate for at most 3.8% of problems, and this paper independently measures 6.11% significance on reference implementations. Our sealed tasks physically cannot carry a timing signal.

## intake-977#record — MLE-bench: Evaluating Machine Learning Agents on Machine Learning Engineering

- **URL**: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07095
- **Verification**: dive-verified
- **Verdict**: worth_investigating (novelty medium, relevance high; categories: benchmark_methodology, agent_architecture)
- **Expanded from**: intake-940#record

### Claims (as verified)

0. LITE IS QUANTIZED AT 1.5152 pp AND THE TOP OF THE BOARD IS A THREE-WAY EXACT TIE. Lite = 22 competitions x 3 seeds = 66 task-runs, so Any-Medal can only take values k/66. Every published Lite number is an integer medal count in disguise: 80.30% = 53/66, 86.36% = 57/66, 90.91% = 60/66, 77.27% = 51/66, 75.76% = 50/66, 71.21% = 47/66.
1. The recurring +-1.52 on the official board is EXACTLY ONE MEDAL OUTCOME of SEM. Three different agents - Famou-Agent 2.0 (24h), MLEvolve (12h), PiEvolve (24h) - all sit at 80.30 +- 1.52. LITE NO LONGER DISCRIMINATES AMONG TOP AGENTS.
2. n=3 IS UNDER-POWERED BY THE SABOTAGE PAPER OWN CITATION: "Because performance on MLE-Bench is high-variance, Chan et al. suggest 3 runs per task, however more recent work suggests a minimum of 10 runs (see appendix H of Toledo et al.)." The entire leaderboard convention is 3.
3. THE VERIFICATION PATH IS CLOSED, with the exact date. README verbatim: "Update (04-24-2026): We are currently not taking any new submissions to the leaderboard while we develop an improved process for ensuring submissions are fair and comparable."
4. ITS OWN AUTHORS NAME THE RESIDUAL RISK. Sec 6: "We have mitigations in place to prevent plagiarism ... but it is difficult to detect the reuse of high-level strategies. Our experiments find no systematic effect of contamination for GPT-4o, but make no guarantees about future models."

### Verdict

worth_investigating: the 21-competition, 41.57 GB sub-split is a feasible LOCAL REGRESSION INSTRUMENT for harness changes. Not adopt_component, because a sub-split score is not an MLE-Bench Lite score and must never be reported as one (1/63 != 1/66).

### Linked handoffs and actions

- updated `handoffs/active/architect-model-selection-bench.md`
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] ADOPT A GOVERNING CITATION RULE: any MLE-Bench number from intake-940#record or intake-413#record must be cited with its integer medal count out of 66, with the +-1.5152 pp quantum stated, and flagged leaderboard-verified or self-reported. NO MARGIN UNDER ~3 MEDAL OUTCOMES (4.5 pp) MAY BE DESCRIBED AS A DIFFERENCE. Neither entry numbers may gate keep/revert/promote without local re-measurement.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] DECLINE a 3-seed local Lite eval - does not fit on disk, 33-66 days of exclusive MI210, and unsubmittable.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] CONDITIONAL GO on the 21-competition 41.57 GB sub-split as a local regression instrument for harness changes, explicitly NEVER reported as an MLE-Bench Lite score.
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] PREREQUISITE: free >=60 GB on /mnt/raid0 before any MLE-Bench-shaped work.

EPYC kernel-search transfer remains an untested hypothesis. 2026-10-06 IRV native-slot mapping audit: Transport wrapper already correct; verify same native slot identity and current supported scope. Original authoritative native key_claims (unchanged historical text): ["ID CORRECTION, recorded so the wrong pointer is not re-derived: MLEvolve is arXiv 2606.06473 (Du et al., Shanghai AI Laboratory, 2026-06-04). arXiv 2606.11926 is a DIFFERENT paper - see intake-981#record.", "IT IS ON THE OFFICIAL LEADERBOARD, PRE-FREEZE, THIRD-PARTY ACCEPTED. Row dated 2026-02-14: Gemini-3-Pro-Preview, Lite 80.30 +- 1.52, Medium 57.89, High 42.22, All 61.33 +- 1.33, 12h, source available, grading reports available. THE ONLY TOP-10 MAINLINE ENTRY WITH SOURCE CODE AVAILABLE. Licence Apache 2.0.", "THE PAPER NUMBERS ARE NOT THE LEADERBOARD NUMBERS. The paper reports All 65.3 +- 0.8 on a DIFFERENT backbone (Gemini-3.1-Pro-preview), post-freeze and unverifiable. Only Lite (80.3) is common to both. CITE 61.33 All (verified), NEVER 65.3 (self-reported).", "UNDISCLOSED HARDWARE DEVIATION: the paper used \"21 vCPUs, 234 GB RAM, and a single NVIDIA H200\"; the official environment is 36 vCPUs, 440 GB RAM, one 24 GB A10. That is roughly 6x the GPU memory at 58% the vCPUs, and the paper does not acknowledge the deviation.", "THE PORTABLE METHOD, and nothing in it requires the H200 or Gemini: progressive MCGS (Monte Carlo GRAPH Search) with cross-branch REFERENCE EDGES rather than tree isolation; an entropy-inspired progressive explore-to-exploit annealing schedule; Retrospective Memory (static KB plus dynamic global memory with hybrid lexical-semantic retrieval); and planning decoupled from code generation. Apache-2.0, CUDA optional."]. Original historical true refutations and dependencies remain preserved; narrowed current effects apply only to bounded replacement statements, never historical fabricated assertions.

### Verdict

The identifier and older official leaderboard row are verified. Keep the 61.33 All score distinct from the paper’s 65.3 All result on a different backbone. Paper hardware differs from MLE-Bench recommended defaults, which are not strict requirements; no compute-match is established. Current Apache-2.0 repo documents a CPU option only for memory embeddings while defaults include CUDA. Leeroo is also marked source-available on the pinned main table, overturning the exclusivity claim. EPYC kernel-search transfer remains an untested hypothesis.
adopt_patterns: Apache-2.0 and CUDA-optional, but specialized for Kaggle-style MLE program synthesis rather than anything in our serving path. The mechanisms transfer; the harness does not.

- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] CITE 61.33 All (leaderboard-verified) and NEVER 65.3 (self-reported on a different backbone, post-freeze).

## intake-987#record — Cirron - hardware performance counters from Python, with two defects that reproduce our own fail-open anti-pattern inside the instrument

- **URL**: https://github.com/s7nfo/Cirron
- **Verification**: dive-verified
- **Verdict**: not_applicable (novelty medium, relevance medium; categories: benchmark_methodology)
- **Expanded from**: intake-971#record

### Claims (as verified)

0. ALL FOUR FLAG SEMANTICS CONFIRMED FROM SOURCE.
1. DEFECT 1: TWO FILE DESCRIPTORS LEAKED PER MEASUREMENT (opens 3, closes 1). A search loop over thousands of candidates exhausts the fd limit.
2. DEFECT 2: end.restype = None makes the Python error check DEAD, so a failed measurement returns instruction_count=0 SILENTLY - our own fail-open anti-pattern, INSIDE THE INSTRUMENT.
3. Adding counters requires surfacing time_running first.
4. Unmaintained since 2024-09-10.

### Verdict

not_applicable as shipped: two defects, both of which bite precisely under automated use, and unmaintained since 2024. Recorded rather than adopted so the defects are known if a future session reaches for it.

### Linked handoffs and actions

- updated `handoffs/active/architect-model-selection-bench.md`
- Recommended action (dive proposal, not an instruction): [review candidate] DO NOT USE AS SHIPPED. If instruction counting is ever needed for generated-code scoring, fix the fd leak and the dead error check first, and never point it at a bandwidth-bound kernel.

- **Expanded from**: intake-938#record

### Claims (as verified)

0. THE SINGLE MOST STACK-RELEVANT NUMBER IN THE MEMORY CLUSTER, and it is in an ablation the citing paper never reports. EvolveR Table 4, Qwen2.5-3B: principle distillation + retrieval with RL DISABLED scores 0.357, BEATING RL-only at 0.325, and reaching 93.5% of the full system 0.382.
1. That is the same shape as our own adopted ReasoningBank baseline, and it is a GRADIENT-FREE WIN - directly portable to an inference-only host.
2. IT NEVER EVALUATES ON ALFWorld OR WebShop. It is a 7-benchmark multi-hop QA paper. "70.1" appears ZERO times; ALFWorld and WebShop appear ZERO times; the only "embodied" mention is in Future Work.
3. CONSEQUENCE FOR intake-938#record: MemHarness "EvolveR (reproduced) 70.1" IS NOT A REPRODUCTION OF ANY PUBLISHED NUMBER - there is no original to diverge from. The label "(reproduced)" asserts fidelity to a non-existent original.
4. NON-INDEPENDENCE: 8 SHARED AUTHORS with MemHarness including the same first author (Rong Wu) and the same seniors; both Shanghai AI Lab.

### Verdict

adopt_patterns: the distill-then-retrieve arm is what transfers; the RL half is out of reach and, per its own ablation, not where most of the value is.

## intake-990#record — The Dark Room in the Reward Channel: dense prediction rewards collapse GRPO-trained agents - and why it does NOT threaten MemHarness

- **URL**: https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21273
- **Verification**: dive-verified
- **Verdict**: worth_investigating (novelty high, relevance medium; categories: training_distillation, agent_architecture, benchmark_methodology)
- **Expanded from**: intake-938#record

### Claims (as verified)

0. THE COLLAPSE MECHANISM DOES NOT REACH MemHarness, and the reason is specific. The mechanism requires a DENSE policy-dependent potential-difference shaping term with persistent within-group variance. MemHarness has sparse +10/0 plus a SATURATING 0.1x format bonus plus SFT format cold-start, which places it in Dark Room own Proposition-2 SAFE class.
   - *unaffected*: v2 (2026-08-04) STRENGTHENS this. Appendix E states the failure regime in our own terms ('a dense policy-dependent shaping term whose spread dominates the within-group variance'), and a new knock-out matrix makes removing the signal one of three sufficient escapes. Verified against full text of v1 and v2, 2026-08-10.
1. DECISIVELY: Dark Room own NON-COLLAPSING BASELINE IS MemHarness reward shape, on the same verl-agent framework.
   - *unaffected*: v2 STRENGTHENS this: the ALFWorld baseline matching MemHarness's reward shape is unchanged at 49.5% and is upgraded from single-seed to seeds 0/42/96. v2 also analyses the invalid-action penalty at coefficient 0.1 that both carry, which v1 only mentioned in passing.
2. BUT IT DOES DESTABILISE MemHarness ON MEASUREMENT: 32-episode binomial noise floor is roughly 8.8 points; the paper states "all cross-arm comparisons in this version are DESCRIPTIVE"; and it reports an unexplained 8B (32.8) scoring below 4B (49.5).
   - *narrowed*: NARROWED by v2. The ~8.8pt binomial noise floor at 32 episodes SURVIVES verbatim. The other two legs do not: v1's blanket caveat that 'all cross-arm comparisons in this version are descriptive' was scoped to v1 and is replaced by unified 140-game formal endpoints, and the unexplained 8B inversion (32.8 vs 4B 49.5) resolves to 45.0% [37.0, 53.3] — a worked example of the very noise floor this claim names. Keep the floor, drop the caveat and the inversion.

### Verdict

worth_investigating: the theory is adoptable as a filter but the empirical arm is self-declared descriptive, and our own use case (a search loop, not a trained policy) sits outside its scope.
